+_DEFAULT_VRAM_BYTES = {
+    ("qwen3.6-35b-a3b", "fp8"): 22_548 * 1024**2,
+    ("qwen3.6-35b-a3b", "nvfp4"): 25 * 1024**3 // 2,
+    # RTX 5090 D v2 三轮实测以 21,592 MiB 为当前峰值，再保留 256 MiB
+    # backend 预算余量；全局 safety 仍由 ResourceManager 额外扣除。
+    # FP8 峰值超过 30.2 GiB，在 24 GiB 卡上只会触发 WDDM 超额驻留。
+    ("qwen3.8-27b", "fp8"): 31 * 1024**3,
+    ("qwen3.8-27b", "nvfp4"): 21_848 * 1024**2,
+    # 实验预算来自权重 header，尚未通过整模型峰值验收。
+    ("qwen3.8-flash-next", "nvfp4"): 22 * 1024**3,
+    # 1024 个 Q2_0 cache slots：完整引擎实测 reserved 约 12 GiB，预留到 14 GiB。
+    ("qwen3.8-flash-next", "q2_0"): 14 * 1024**3,
+}
+_DEFAULT_HOST_BYTES = {
+    ("qwen3.6-35b-a3b", "fp8"): 56 * 1024**3,
+    ("qwen3.6-35b-a3b", "nvfp4"): 24 * 1024**3,
+    # Windows commit_peak 已单独计入显存映射；HTTP 实测额外 host 增量小于
+    # 6 GiB，保留 8 GiB 作为 checkpoint/tokenizer/runtime 的严格预算。
+    ("qwen3.8-27b", "fp8"): 8 * 1024**3,
+    ("qwen3.8-27b", "nvfp4"): 8 * 1024**3,
+    ("qwen3.8-flash-next", "nvfp4"): 70 * 1024**3,
+    # 完整权重 HTTP/逻辑题 RSS 峰值约 35.46 GiB，4K 输入/取消复用也已通过。
+    # 默认 1024 槽、8192 KV、1024 prefill 下取 36 GiB；非默认几何可显式扩预算。
+    ("qwen3.8-flash-next", "q2_0"): 36 * 1024**3,
+}

+class FreeTokenBackend(Backend):
+    """在同一 Speed 环境中管理 Qwen3.x FreeToken worker 的 backend。"""
+
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self._state = BackendState.NOT_LOADED
+        self._spec: ModelSpec | None = None
+        self._cfg: BackendConfig | None = None
+        self._checkpoint: FreeTokenCheckpoint | None = None
+        self._options: _RuntimeOptions | None = None
+        self._cache_identity: KernelCacheIdentity | None = None
+        self._transport: _WorkerTransport | None = None
+        self._worker_runtime_pid: int | None = None
+        self._vram_bytes = 0
+        self._cache_geometry: dict[str, int] = {}
+        self._expert_storage: dict[str, Any] = {}
+        self._mtp_identity: dict[str, Any] = {}
+        self._restart_required = False
+        self._lock = threading.RLock()
+        self._request_lock = threading.Lock()
+
+    @property
+    def state(self) -> BackendState:
+        return self._state
+
+    @property
+    def worker_pid(self) -> int | None:
+        transport = self._transport
+        if transport is None or transport.process.poll() is not None:
+            return None
+        return self._worker_runtime_pid
+
+    def cache_geometry(self) -> dict[str, int]:
+        """返回 worker 已解析并实际分配的运行时 cache 几何。"""
+
+        with self._lock:
+            return dict(self._cache_geometry)
+
+    def expert_storage(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """返回 worker 实际专家 bank 容量，供运行验收使用。"""
+        with self._lock:
+            return dict(self._expert_storage)
+
+    def mtp_identity(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """返回已核验的真实 MTP 所有者回执，供开关与资源验收使用。"""
+        with self._lock:
+            return dict(self._mtp_identity)
+
+    @classmethod
+    def vram_estimate_before_load(
+        cls,
+        spec: ModelSpec,
+        cfg: BackendConfig,
+    ) -> int:
+        checkpoint = _checkpoint_for(spec, cfg)
+        return _runtime_options(checkpoint, cfg).vram_estimate_bytes
+
+    @classmethod
+    def estimate_load_cost(
+        cls,
+        spec: ModelSpec,
+        cfg: BackendConfig,
+    ) -> LoadCost:
+        """按 profile 与量化格式的进程峰值执行严格 Windows admission。"""
+
+        checkpoint = _checkpoint_for(spec, cfg)
+        options = _runtime_options(checkpoint, cfg)
+        return LoadCost(
+            model_id=spec.model_id,
+            vram_peak=options.vram_estimate_bytes,
+            commit_peak=(options.vram_estimate_bytes if os.name == "nt" else 0),
+            cpu_heap_peak=options.host_commit_estimate_bytes,
+            weight_files=[
+                str(checkpoint.root / name) for name in checkpoint.shard_names
+            ],
+            reason=(
+                f"FreeToken native worker for {checkpoint.profile_label} "
+                f"{checkpoint.quant_format.upper()} "
+                f"{'expert cache' if checkpoint.is_moe else 'dense runtime'}"
+            ),
+            strict_commit_limit=True,
+        )
+
+    @classmethod
+    def compat_check(
+        cls,
+        spec: ModelSpec,
+        hw: HardwareInfo,
+        cfg: BackendConfig,
+    ) -> CompatResult:
+        """在权重加载前冻结 checkpoint、环境、AOT cache 与资源要求。"""
+
+        try:
+            checkpoint = _checkpoint_for(spec, cfg)
+            options = _runtime_options(checkpoint, cfg)
+            if sys.version_info[:2] != PYTHON_MINOR:
+                raise ValueError("FreeToken Qwen3.x requires CPython 3.12")
+            verify_kernel_cache(options.kernel_cache)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return CompatResult(ok=False, reason=str(exc))
+
+        if hw.cuda_capability != CUDA_CAPABILITY:
+            return CompatResult(
+                ok=False,
+                reason="FreeToken Qwen3.x requires an exact SM120 CUDA GPU",
+                vram_required_bytes=options.vram_estimate_bytes,
+                ram_required_bytes=options.host_commit_estimate_bytes,
+            )
+        if options.vram_estimate_bytes > hw.vram_total_bytes:
+            return CompatResult(
+                ok=False,
+                reason=(
+                    f"FreeToken {checkpoint.profile_label} profile needs about "
+                    f"{options.vram_estimate_bytes / 1024**3:.2f}GiB VRAM"
+                ),
+                vram_required_bytes=options.vram_estimate_bytes,
+                ram_required_bytes=options.host_commit_estimate_bytes,
+            )
+        if (
+            hw.ram_total_bytes > 0
+            and options.host_commit_estimate_bytes > hw.ram_total_bytes
+        ):
+            return CompatResult(
+                ok=False,
+                reason=(
+                    f"FreeToken {checkpoint.profile_label} profile needs about "
+                    f"{options.host_commit_estimate_bytes / 1024**3:.0f}GiB host memory"
+                ),
+                vram_required_bytes=options.vram_estimate_bytes,
+                ram_required_bytes=options.host_commit_estimate_bytes,
+            )
+        return CompatResult(
+            ok=True,
+            vram_required_bytes=options.vram_estimate_bytes,
+            ram_required_bytes=options.host_commit_estimate_bytes,
+        )
+
+    @classmethod
+    def preflight(
+        cls,
+        spec: ModelSpec,
+        cfg: BackendConfig,
+        user_params: dict,
+        cancel_event: object | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        """加载模型前完成文本、采样和 prompt+output 预算检查。"""
+
+        _check_cancel_event(cancel_event)
+        checkpoint = _checkpoint_for(spec, cfg)
+        options = _runtime_options(checkpoint, cfg)
+        messages, enable_thinking = _validate_inputs(user_params)
+        sampling = _sampling_values(
+            checkpoint,
+            enable_thinking=enable_thinking,
+            values=user_params,
+        )
+        if sampling["max_tokens"] > options.max_output_tokens:
+            raise BackendInputError(
+                "max_tokens exceeds this FreeToken Qwen3.x profile output limit",
+                code="max_output_tokens_exceeded",
+                param="max_tokens",
+            )
+        tokenizer = _load_tokenizer(checkpoint, cancel_event)
+        prompt_tokens = _apply_chat_template(
+            tokenizer,
+            messages,
+            enable_thinking=enable_thinking,
+        )
+        _validate_total_budget(len(prompt_tokens), sampling["max_tokens"])
+        # 固定KV池会把引擎实际上下文上限收紧；不可能生成的prompt必须在大权重加载前拒绝。
+        # 原生引擎仍可按剩余空间缩短max_tokens，这里只检查至少一个输出token能否容纳。
+        if options.kv_cache_tokens is not None and len(prompt_tokens) >= min(
+            options.max_seq_len, options.kv_cache_tokens
+        ):
+            raise BackendInputError(
+                "Qwen3.x prompt exceeds the configured KV cache context window",
+                code="context_length_exceeded",
+            )
+        _check_cancel_event(cancel_event)
+
+    def load(
+        self,
+        spec: ModelSpec,
+        cfg: BackendConfig,
+        cancel_event: object | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        """启动同解释器 worker，并等待精确 PID/executable/cache ready 回执。"""
+
+        with self._lock:
+            if self._state == BackendState.LOADED:
+                if self._spec == spec:
+                    return
+                raise InvalidStateError(
+                    "FreeToken Qwen backend is loaded with a different ModelSpec"
+                )
+            if self._state != BackendState.NOT_LOADED:
+                raise InvalidStateError(
+                    f"Cannot load FreeToken Qwen backend in state {self._state}"
+                )
+            self._state = BackendState.LOADING
+
+        try:
+            _check_cancel_event(cancel_event)
+            checkpoint = _checkpoint_for(spec, cfg)
+            options = _runtime_options(checkpoint, cfg)
+            cache_identity = verify_kernel_cache(options.kernel_cache)
+            with self._lock:
+                self._spec = spec
+                self._cfg = cfg
+                self._checkpoint = checkpoint
+                self._options = options
+                self._cache_identity = cache_identity
+                self._start_worker(cancel_event=cancel_event)
+                self._state = BackendState.LOADED
+        except BaseException:
+            with self._lock:
+                self._stop_worker(graceful=False)
+                self._clear_model_state()
+                self._state = BackendState.NOT_LOADED
+            raise
+
+    def unload(self) -> None:
+        """在单一总期限内请求 shutdown，随后回收 worker 完整进程树。"""
+
+        with self._lock:
+            if self._state == BackendState.NOT_LOADED:
+                return
+            if self._state == BackendState.LOADING:
+                raise InvalidStateError(
+                    "Cannot unload FreeToken Qwen backend while LOADING"
+                )
+            self._state = BackendState.UNLOADING
+            try:
+                self._stop_worker(graceful=True)
+            finally:
+                self._clear_model_state()
+                self._state = BackendState.NOT_LOADED
+
+    def generate(self, inputs: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Iterator[Any]:
+        """以 25ms 轮询消费 token；取消经同一 pipe 注入并等待 terminal ack。"""
+
+        with self._request_lock:
+            yield from self._generate_single(inputs, **kwargs)
+
+    def _generate_single(self, inputs: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Iterator[Any]:
+        """串行一个请求，避免多个消费者从同一响应管道交叉取帧。"""
+
+        cancel_check = kwargs.get("cancel_check")
+        _check_cancel_callback(cancel_check)
+        messages, enable_thinking = _validate_inputs(inputs)
+        with self._lock:
+            if self._state != BackendState.LOADED:
+                raise InvalidStateError(
+                    f"FreeToken Qwen backend is not loaded; state={self._state}"
+                )
+            self._ensure_worker(cancel_check=cancel_check)
+            checkpoint = self._checkpoint
+            options = self._options
+            transport = self._transport
+        if checkpoint is None or options is None or transport is None:
+            raise InvalidStateError("FreeToken worker identity is missing")
+
+        sampling = _sampling_values(
+            checkpoint,
+            enable_thinking=enable_thinking,
+            values=kwargs,
+        )
+        if sampling["max_tokens"] > options.max_output_tokens:
+            raise BackendInputError(
+                "max_tokens exceeds this FreeToken Qwen3.x profile output limit",
+                code="max_output_tokens_exceeded",
+                param="max_tokens",
+            )
+        request_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
+        command = {
+            "type": "generate",
+            "request_id": request_id,
+            "messages": messages,
+            "enable_thinking": enable_thinking,
+            "sampling": sampling,
+        }
+        tokens: list[int] = []
+        terminal = False
+        try:
+            transport.send(command)
+            deadline = time.monotonic() + options.request_timeout_s
+            while True:
+                try:
+                    if cancel_check is not None and bool(cancel_check()):
+                        self._cancel_and_raise(
+                            transport,
+                            request_id,
+                            options.cancel_settle_timeout_s,
+                        )
+                except PipelineCancellation:
+                    raise
+                except Exception as exc:
+                    settled = self._cancel_request(
+                        transport,
+                        request_id,
+                        options.cancel_settle_timeout_s,
+                    )
+                    if not settled:
+                        self._invalidate_worker()
+                    raise BackendInferenceError(
+                        "FreeToken cancellation callback failed"
+                    ) from exc
+
+                remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
+                if remaining <= 0:
+                    raise BackendInferenceError(
+                        "FreeToken generation exceeded its request deadline"
+                    )
+                try:
+                    message = transport.receive(min(_POLL_SECONDS, remaining))
+                except TimeoutError:
+                    continue
+                message_type = message.get("type")
+                if message_type == "fatal":
+                    raise BackendInferenceError(
+                        "FreeToken worker stopped after an internal failure"
+                    )
+                _require_request_message(message, request_id)
+                if message_type == "token":
+                    token_id = _non_negative_int(message, "token_id")
+                    tokens.append(token_id)
+                    yield {
+                        "type": "token",
+                        "token_id": token_id,
+                        "delta_reasoning": _text_field(message, "delta_reasoning"),
+                        "delta_text": _text_field(message, "delta_text"),
+                        "is_final_token": _bool_field(message, "is_final_token"),
+                    }
+                    continue
+                if message_type == "delta":
+                    yield {
+                        "type": "delta",
+                        "delta_reasoning": _text_field(message, "delta_reasoning"),
+                        "delta_text": _text_field(message, "delta_text"),
+                    }
+                    continue
+                if message_type == "request_error":
+                    if message.get("code") != "context_length_exceeded":
+                        raise WireProtocolError(
+                            "FreeToken worker returned an unknown request error"
+                        )
+                    terminal = True
+                    raise BackendInputError(
+                        _request_error_message(message),
+                        code="context_length_exceeded",
+                    )
+                if message_type == "cancelled":
+                    terminal = True
+                    raise PipelineCancellation()
+                if message_type == "final":
+                    terminal = True
+                    yield _final_projection(message, tokens)
+                    return
+                raise WireProtocolError(
+                    f"unexpected FreeToken worker message {message_type!r}"
+                )
+        except GeneratorExit:
+            if not terminal and not self._cancel_request(
+                transport,
+                request_id,
+                options.cancel_settle_timeout_s,
+            ):
+                self._invalidate_worker()
+            raise
+        except PipelineCancellation:
+            terminal = True
+            _raise_cancelled()
+        except BackendInputError:
+            raise
+        except BackendInferenceError:
+            self._invalidate_worker()
+            raise
+        except (ConnectionError, OSError, WireProtocolError) as exc:
+            self._invalidate_worker()
+            raise BackendInferenceError("FreeToken worker transport failed") from exc
+
+    def vram_current(self) -> int:
+        if not self._lock.acquire(timeout=_STATUS_LOCK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS):
+            options = self._options
+            estimate = options.vram_estimate_bytes if options is not None else 0
+            return max(self._vram_bytes, estimate)
+        try:
+            if self._state != BackendState.LOADED:
+                return 0
+            transport = self._transport
+            if transport is None or transport.process.poll() is not None:
+                self._restart_required = True
+                self._vram_bytes = 0
+                return 0
+            return self._vram_bytes
+        finally:
+            self._lock.release()
+
+    def supports_feature(self, name: str) -> bool:
+        return name in {
+            "fp8",
+            "nvfp4",
+            "streaming",
+            "process_isolation",
+            "native_ipc",
+        }
+
+    def _start_worker(
+        self,
+        *,
+        cancel_event: object | None = None,
+        cancel_check: Callable[[], bool] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        """创建精确 Speed 解释器进程，并在线性 deadline 内完成双握手。"""
+
+        checkpoint = self._checkpoint
+        options = self._options
+        cache_identity = self._cache_identity
+        if checkpoint is None or options is None or cache_identity is None:
+            raise InvalidStateError("FreeToken model configuration is missing")
+        if self._transport is not None:
+            raise InvalidStateError("FreeToken worker process already exists")
+
+        environment = os.environ.copy()
+        # 正常模型不能继承研究进程的专家复用开关，避免加载时悄悄改变权重。
+        environment.pop("FREETOKEN_ALLOW_INVALID_MODEL", None)
+        environment.pop("FREETOKEN_INVALID_EXPERT_KEEP_LAYERS", None)
+        environment["PYTHONNOUSERSITE"] = "1"
+        environment["FREETOKEN_DISABLE_JIT"] = "1"
+        environment["FREETOKEN_KERNEL_CACHE_DIR"] = str(options.kernel_cache)
+        runtime_dir = get_config().freetoken_runtime_dir
+        if runtime_dir:
+            runtime_path = str(Path(runtime_dir).expanduser().resolve())
+            environment["SILITOK_FREETOKEN_RUNTIME_DIR"] = runtime_path
+            inherited = environment.get("PYTHONPATH")
+            environment["PYTHONPATH"] = (
+                runtime_path + os.pathsep + inherited if inherited else runtime_path
+            )
+        environment.pop("FREETOKEN_DISABLE_VERSION_CHECK", None)
+        environment["CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"] = _worker_cuda_visibility(
+            options.gpu_index,
+            environment.get("CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"),
+        )
+        creationflags = 0
+        popen_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {}
+        if os.name == "nt":
+            creationflags = getattr(
+                subprocess, "CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP", 0
+            ) | getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0)
+        else:
+            popen_kwargs["start_new_session"] = True
+        try:
+            process = subprocess.Popen(
+                _worker_command(),
+                stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
+                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
+                stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
+                cwd=str(checkpoint.root),
+                env=environment,
+                close_fds=True,
+                bufsize=0,
+                creationflags=creationflags,
+                **popen_kwargs,
+            )
+        except OSError as exc:
+            raise ModelCorruptError("FreeToken worker could not start") from exc
+        transport = _WorkerTransport(process)
+        self._transport = transport
+        self._vram_bytes = options.vram_estimate_bytes
+        self._restart_required = False
+        deadline = time.monotonic() + options.startup_timeout_s
+
+        hello = self._wait_startup_message(
+            transport,
+            deadline,
+            cancel_event=cancel_event,
+            cancel_check=cancel_check,
+        )
+        expected_executable = os.path.normcase(str(Path(sys.executable).resolve()))
+        observed_executable = hello.get("python_executable")
+        observed_pid = hello.get("pid")
+        if (
+            hello.get("type") != "hello"
+            or isinstance(observed_pid, bool)
+            or not isinstance(observed_pid, int)
+            or not _process_tree_contains_pid(process, observed_pid)
+            or not isinstance(observed_executable, str)
+            or os.path.normcase(str(Path(observed_executable).resolve()))
+            != expected_executable
+        ):
+            raise ModelCorruptError(
+                "FreeToken worker returned the wrong process identity"
+            )
+        self._worker_runtime_pid = observed_pid
+
+        transport.send(
+            {
+                "type": "load",
+                "model_path": str(checkpoint.root),
+                "kernel_cache": str(options.kernel_cache),
+                "options": _worker_option_payload(options),
+            }
+        )
+        ready = self._wait_startup_message(
+            transport,
+            deadline,
+            cancel_event=cancel_event,
+            cancel_check=cancel_check,
+        )
+        if (
+            ready.get("type") != "ready"
+            or ready.get("kernel_artifacts") != cache_identity.artifact_count
+        ):
+            raise ModelCorruptError(
+                "FreeToken worker returned the wrong kernel cache artifact set"
+            )
+        observed_vram = ready.get("vram_bytes")
+        if isinstance(observed_vram, int) and not isinstance(observed_vram, bool):
+            self._vram_bytes = max(0, observed_vram)
+        observed_geometry = ready.get("cache_geometry")
+        if isinstance(observed_geometry, dict) and all(
+            isinstance(key, str)
+            and isinstance(value, int)
+            and not isinstance(value, bool)
+            and value >= 0
+            for key, value in observed_geometry.items()
+        ):
+            self._cache_geometry = dict(observed_geometry)
+        storage = ready.get("expert_storage")
+        geometry = checkpoint.expert_geometry
+        resident = options.moe_q2_resident_experts
+        per_expert = (
+            geometry["expert_bytes"]
+            // (geometry["layers"] * geometry["experts_per_layer"])
+            if geometry
+            else 0
+        )
+        expected_host = (
+            geometry["expert_bytes"] - resident * per_expert if geometry else 0
+        )
+        if checkpoint.quant_format == "q2_0" and (
+            not isinstance(storage, dict)
+            or storage.get("format") != "q2_0"
+            or geometry is None
+            or storage.get("layers") != geometry["layers"]
+            or storage.get("experts_per_layer") != geometry["experts_per_layer"]
+            or storage.get("host_bytes") != expected_host
+            or storage.get("all_pinned") is not True
+            or (
+                resident
+                and (
+                    storage.get("protected_experts") != resident
+                    or storage.get("dynamic_slots") != options.moe_q2_dynamic_slots
+                    or storage.get("protected_gpu_bytes") != resident * per_expert
+                    or storage.get("gpu_cache_bytes")
+                    != options.moe_cache_size * per_expert
+                )
+            )
+        ):
+            raise ModelCorruptError(
+                "Q2_0 worker did not load the complete compressed expert ownership set"
+            )
+        if isinstance(storage, dict):
+            self._expert_storage = dict(storage)
+        expected_mtp = expected_mtp_ready(
+            options.mtp_manifest_sha256,
+            compact_state=options.mtp_compact_state,
+            state_cache_slots=options.linear_state_cache_slots,
+            max_prefill_length=options.max_prefill_length,
+            draft_policy=options.mtp_draft_policy,
+        )
+        if ready.get("mtp") != expected_mtp:
+            logger.error(
+                "MTP ready mismatch: expected=%r actual=%r",
+                expected_mtp,
+                ready.get("mtp"),
+            )
+            differences = {
+                key: (value, (ready.get("mtp") or {}).get(key))
+                for key, value in expected_mtp.items()
+                if (ready.get("mtp") or {}).get(key) != value
+            }
+            raise ModelCorruptError(
+                f"FreeToken worker MTP ownership differs from the release contract: {differences}"
+            )
+        self._mtp_identity = dict(ready["mtp"])
+
+    def _wait_startup_message(
+        self,
+        transport: _WorkerTransport,
+        deadline: float,
+        *,
+        cancel_event: object | None,
+        cancel_check: Callable[[], bool] | None,
+    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        while True:
+            _check_cancel_event(cancel_event)
+            _check_cancel_callback(cancel_check)
+            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
+            if remaining <= 0:
+                self._log_worker_failure("startup timeout")
+                raise ModelCorruptError(
+                    "FreeToken worker did not become ready before its deadline"
+                )
+            try:
+                message = transport.receive(min(0.1, remaining))
+            except TimeoutError:
+                if transport.process.poll() is not None:
+                    self._log_worker_failure("startup")
+                    raise ModelCorruptError("FreeToken worker exited during startup")
+                continue
+            except ConnectionError as exc:
+                self._log_worker_failure("startup")
+                raise ModelCorruptError(
+                    "FreeToken worker failed during startup"
+                ) from exc
+            if message.get("type") == "fatal":
+                self._log_worker_failure("startup")
+                raise ModelCorruptError(
+                    "FreeToken worker rejected the model or runtime"
+                )
+            return message
+
+    def _ensure_worker(
+        self,
+        *,
+        cancel_check: Callable[[], bool] | None,
+    ) -> None:
+        transport = self._transport
+        if (
+            not self._restart_required
+            and transport is not None
+            and transport.process.poll() is None
+        ):
+            return
+        _check_cancel_callback(cancel_check)
+        self._stop_worker(graceful=False)
+        self._restart_required = True
+        try:
+            self._start_worker(cancel_check=cancel_check)
+        except BaseException:
+            self._stop_worker(graceful=False)
+            self._restart_required = True
+            raise
+
+    def _stop_worker(self, *, graceful: bool) -> None:
+        """在一个 deadline 内完成协议退出、强制树回收与管道关闭。"""
+
+        transport = self._transport
+        options = self._options
+        timeout_s = options.shutdown_timeout_s if options is not None else 10.0
+        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_s
+        if transport is not None and transport.process.poll() is None and graceful:
+            try:
+                transport.send({"type": "shutdown"})
+                while time.monotonic() < deadline:
+                    try:
+                        message = transport.receive(
+                            min(0.1, deadline - time.monotonic())
+                        )
+                    except TimeoutError:
+                        if transport.process.poll() is not None:
+                            break
+                        continue
+                    if message.get("type") == "stopped":
+                        break
+            except (ConnectionError, OSError, WireProtocolError):
+                logger.warning(
+                    "FreeToken worker graceful shutdown failed; forcing cleanup"
+                )
+        if transport is not None:
+            _terminate_owned_process(
+                transport.process,
+                timeout_s=max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()),
+            )
+            transport.close_streams()
+        self._transport = None
+        self._worker_runtime_pid = None
+        self._vram_bytes = 0
+        self._cache_geometry = {}
+        self._expert_storage = {}
+        self._mtp_identity = {}
+        self._restart_required = False
+
+    def _invalidate_worker(self) -> None:
+        with self._lock:
+            self._log_worker_failure("request")
+            self._stop_worker(graceful=False)
+            self._restart_required = True
+
+    def _cancel_request(
+        self,
+        transport: _WorkerTransport,
+        request_id: str,
+        timeout_s: float,
+    ) -> bool:
+        """发送一次取消并消费到该请求终态；失败时调用方必须重建 worker。"""
+
+        try:
+            transport.send({"type": "cancel", "request_id": request_id})
+        except ConnectionError:
+            return False
+        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_s
+        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
+            try:
+                message = transport.receive(
+                    min(_POLL_SECONDS, deadline - time.monotonic())
+                )
+            except TimeoutError:
+                continue
+            except ConnectionError:
+                return False
+            if message.get("type") == "fatal":
+                return False
+            try:
+                _require_request_message(message, request_id)
+            except WireProtocolError:
+                return False
+            if message.get("type") in {"cancelled", "final", "request_error"}:
+                return True
+            if message.get("type") not in {"token", "delta"}:
+                return False
+        return False
+
+    def _cancel_and_raise(
+        self,
+        transport: _WorkerTransport,
+        request_id: str,
+        timeout_s: float,
+    ) -> None:
+        if not self._cancel_request(transport, request_id, timeout_s):
+            self._invalidate_worker()
+        raise PipelineCancellation()
+
+    def _log_worker_failure(self, phase: str) -> None:
+        transport = self._transport
+        if transport is None:
+            return
+        tail = transport.stderr_tail().strip()
+        if tail:
+            logger.error("FreeToken worker %s failure tail:\n%s", phase, tail)
+
+    def _clear_model_state(self) -> None:
+        self._spec = None
+        self._cfg = None
+        self._checkpoint = None
+        self._options = None
+        self._cache_identity = None
+        self._worker_runtime_pid = None
+        self._restart_required = False
+
+
+class PipelineCancellation(Exception):
+    """仅用于不在模块导入期拉入 pipeline 包的本地控制流标记。"""
+
+
+def _raise_cancelled(cause: BaseException | None = None) -> None:
+    from silitok_speed.pipelines.base import PipelineCancelledError
+
+    # 外层只需识别真实 pipeline 异常；本地别名让 generate 的异常分支保持窄。
+    if cause is None:
+        raise PipelineCancelledError()
+    raise PipelineCancelledError() from cause
+
+
+def _checkpoint_for(spec: ModelSpec, cfg: BackendConfig) -> FreeTokenCheckpoint:
+    """所有模型校验完整权重来源，拒绝缺失或旧实验 checkpoint。"""
+    try:
+        return inspect_freetoken_checkpoint(spec.weights_path, spec.quantization)
+    except FreeTokenCheckpointError as exc:
+        raise ModelCorruptError(str(exc)) from exc
+
+
+def _runtime_options(
+    checkpoint: FreeTokenCheckpoint,
+    cfg: BackendConfig,
+) -> _RuntimeOptions:
+    """把 manifest 入口和 FreeToken 调优字段收敛为一个启动合同。"""
+
+    if cfg.offload:
+        raise ValueError(
+            "FreeToken Qwen backends keep engine.offload=false; "
+            "FreeToken owns the model residency policy"
+        )
+    if cfg.max_batch_size != 1:
+        raise ValueError("FreeToken Qwen3.x supports max_batch_size=1 only")
+    match = re.fullmatch(r"cuda:(\d+)", cfg.device)
+    if match is None:
+        raise ValueError("FreeToken Qwen3.x device must be cuda:<index>")
+    extra = cfg.extra or {}
+    if not isinstance(extra, dict):
+        raise ValueError("FreeToken Qwen3.x engine.extra must be an object")
+    unknown = sorted(set(extra) - _RUNTIME_EXTRA_KEYS)
+    if unknown:
+        raise ValueError(
+            "FreeToken Qwen3.x engine.extra contains unsupported fields: "
+            + ", ".join(unknown)
+        )
+    if not checkpoint.is_moe:
+        dense_noops = sorted(
+            set(extra)
+            & {
+                "expert_cache_pages",
+                "expert_load",
+                "expert_staging_pages",
+                "kv_reserve_tokens",
+                "moe_cache_auto",
+                "moe_cache_size",
+                "moe_q2_resident_experts",
+                "moe_q2_dynamic_slots",
+            }
+        )
+        if dense_noops:
+            raise ValueError(
+                "dense FreeToken Qwen profile does not accept MoE-only fields: "
+                + ", ".join(dense_noops)
+            )
+    cache_path = extra.get("kernel_cache_dir")
+    if cache_path is not None and (
+        not isinstance(cache_path, str) or not cache_path.strip()
+    ):
+        raise ValueError("kernel_cache_dir must be a non-empty path string")
+    configured_cache = get_config().freetoken_kernel_cache_dir
+    cache_path = cache_path or configured_cache
+    kernel_cache = (
+        Path(cache_path).expanduser().resolve()
+        if isinstance(cache_path, str)
+        else kernel_cache_directory()
+    )
+    flash_next = checkpoint.profile == "qwen3.8-flash-next"
+    strata_q2 = checkpoint.weight_layout == "gguf_strata"
+    mtp_enabled = _bool_option(extra, "mtp_enabled")
+    mtp_path = extra.get("mtp_runtime_path")
+    if mtp_path is not None and (not isinstance(mtp_path, str) or not mtp_path.strip()):
+        raise ValueError("mtp_runtime_path must be a non-empty path string")
+    mtp_root, mtp_digest = None, None
+    if mtp_enabled:
+        if not strata_q2 or not flash_next:
+            raise ValueError(
+                "MTP requires the complete Flash Next Strata Q2 checkpoint"
+            )
+        if mtp_path is None:
+            raise ValueError("mtp_enabled=true requires mtp_runtime_path")
+        mtp_root, mtp_digest = inspect_mtp_runtime(mtp_path)
+    resident_default = (7168 if mtp_enabled else 9216) if strata_q2 else 0
+    resident = _int_option(extra, "moe_q2_resident_experts", resident_default, 0, 9216)
+    dynamic = _int_option(extra, "moe_q2_dynamic_slots", 1024, 0, 1024)
+    if not strata_q2 and (
+        "moe_q2_resident_experts" in extra or "moe_q2_dynamic_slots" in extra
+    ):
+        raise ValueError(
+            "Protected Q2 options require the complete Strata GGUF checkpoint"
+        )
+    if mtp_enabled and not resident:
+        raise ValueError("MTP requires protected compressed Q2 experts")
+    compact_state = _bool_option(extra, "mtp_compact_state")
+    if compact_state and not mtp_enabled:
+        raise ValueError("mtp_compact_state requires enabled MTP")
+    draft_policy = extra.get("mtp_draft_policy", "full")
+    if draft_policy not in ("full", "paired_cjk"):
+        raise ValueError("mtp_draft_policy must be full or paired_cjk")
+    if draft_policy != "full" and not mtp_enabled:
+        raise ValueError("mtp_draft_policy=paired_cjk requires enabled MTP")
+    state_cache_slots = extra.get("linear_state_cache_slots")
+    if state_cache_slots is not None:
+        state_cache_slots = _checked_int(
+            "linear_state_cache_slots", state_cache_slots, 0, 65536
+        )
+    adapt_every = _int_option(extra, "moe_q2_adapt_every", 0, 0, 1_000_000)
+    adapt_decay = _float_option(extra, "moe_q2_adapt_decay", 1.0, 0.0, 1.0)
+    if adapt_decay == 0:
+        raise ValueError("moe_q2_adapt_decay must be in (0, 1]")
+    embedding_host = _bool_option(extra, "gguf_embedding_host")
+    if embedding_host and not strata_q2:
+        raise ValueError(
+            "Mapped input embedding requires the complete Strata GGUF checkpoint"
+        )
+    workspace = _bool_option(extra, "moe_q2_prefill_workspace")
+    if workspace and not (strata_q2 and resident and dynamic == 1024):
+        raise ValueError(
+            "Prefill workspace requires protected Strata Q2 and 1024 dynamic rows"
+        )
+    rebuild_reserve = _int_option(extra, "fixed_q2_rebuild_reserve_mib", 512, 0, 512)
+    if rebuild_reserve != 512 and not (strata_q2 and resident):
+        raise ValueError("A custom fixed rebuild reserve requires protected Strata Q2")
+    row_cache_rows = _int_option(extra, "ple_row_cache_rows", 0, 0, 1 << 24)
+    if row_cache_rows and (row_cache_rows < 8 or not flash_next):
+        raise ValueError("PLE row caching requires Flash Next and at least8 rows")
+    if (state_cache_slots is not None or adapt_every) and not (strata_q2 and resident):
+        raise ValueError(
+            "Linear-state tuning and adaptive owners require protected Strata Q2"
+        )
+    if flash_next and checkpoint.quant_format not in {"nvfp4", "q2_0"}:
+        raise ValueError(
+            "Flash Next currently requires the verified RadixArk NVFP4 layout"
+        )
+    memory_default = (
+        0.90
+        if resident
+        else (
+            0.80
+            if checkpoint.is_moe and checkpoint.quant_format in {"nvfp4", "q2_0"}
+            else 0.90
+        )
+    )
+    memory_ratio = _float_option(extra, "memory_ratio", memory_default, 0.1, 0.98)
+    # Windows packaging does not ship a supported FlashInfer wheel.  An old
+    # FlashInfer accidentally present in a development environment can be
+    # imported and then fail on the newer ``backend=`` API, so select Triton
+    # unless the manifest explicitly requests another backend.
+    attention_backend = extra.get(
+        "attention_backend",
+        "qsa_sparse" if flash_next else ("triton" if os.name == "nt" else "auto"),
+    )
+    if attention_backend not in {"auto", "triton", "fi", "fa", "trtllm", "qsa_sparse"}:
+        raise ValueError("attention_backend is invalid")
+    if not flash_next and attention_backend == "qsa_sparse":
+        raise ValueError("qsa_sparse applies only to Flash Next")
+    if flash_next and attention_backend not in {"auto", "qsa_sparse"}:
+        raise ValueError("Flash Next requires attention_backend='qsa_sparse' or 'auto'")
+    ple_backend = extra.get("ple_backend", "disk")
+    if ple_backend not in {"disk", "pinned"}:
+        raise ValueError("ple_backend must be 'disk' or 'pinned'")
+    if row_cache_rows and ple_backend != "disk":
+        raise ValueError("PLE row caching requires ple_backend=disk")
+    if not flash_next and "ple_backend" in extra:
+        raise ValueError("ple_backend applies only to Flash Next")
+    cache_type = extra.get("cache_type", "radix")
+    if cache_type not in {"naive", "radix"}:
+        raise ValueError("cache_type must be 'naive' or 'radix'")
+    max_seq_len = MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS
+    max_output_tokens = MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS
+    max_prefill_length = _int_option(
+        extra,
+        "max_prefill_length",
+        # Q2 分组 GEMM 可遮蔽单轮整层搬运；更大 chunk 避免重复走全专家银行。
+        (4096 if checkpoint.quant_format == "q2_0" else 1024)
+        if flash_next
+        else _DEFAULT_MAX_PREFILL_LENGTH,
+        1,
+        max_seq_len,
+    )
+    kv_reserve_tokens = _int_option(
+        extra,
+        "kv_reserve_tokens",
+        8192 if checkpoint.is_moe else 0,
+        0,
+        65_536,
+    )
+    cuda_graph_max_bs = _int_option(extra, "cuda_graph_max_bs", 1, 0, 64)
+    decode_log_interval = _int_option(extra, "decode_log_interval", 1000, 1, 1_000_000)
+    requested_expert_load = extra.get("expert_load", "auto")
+    if requested_expert_load not in {"auto", "serial", "parallel"}:
+        raise ValueError("expert_load must be 'auto', 'serial', or 'parallel'")
+    if os.name == "nt" and checkpoint.is_moe:
+        if requested_expert_load not in {"auto", "serial"}:
+            raise ValueError("Windows Qwen requires expert_load='serial'")
+        expert_load = "serial"
+    else:
+        expert_load = str(requested_expert_load)
+    nvfp4_backend = extra.get(
+        "nvfp4_backend",
+        "triton" if checkpoint.quant_format == "nvfp4" else "auto",
+    )
+    if nvfp4_backend not in {"auto", "marlin", "flashinfer", "triton"}:
+        raise ValueError("nvfp4_backend is invalid")
+    if "moe_cache_size" in extra and "expert_cache_pages" in extra:
+        raise ValueError(
+            "moe_cache_size and legacy expert_cache_pages cannot both be set"
+        )
+    has_explicit_cache_size = "moe_cache_size" in extra or "expert_cache_pages" in extra
+    has_explicit_cache_auto = "moe_cache_auto" in extra
+    if not checkpoint.is_moe:
+        moe_cache_size_raw = 0
+    elif (
+        checkpoint.quant_format in {"nvfp4", "q2_0"}
+        and not has_explicit_cache_size
+        and not has_explicit_cache_auto
+    ):
+        moe_cache_size_raw = (
+            resident + dynamic + 1024
+            if resident
+            else (1024 if flash_next else _DEFAULT_NVFP4_MOE_CACHE_SIZE)
+        )
+    else:
+        moe_cache_size_raw = extra.get(
+            "moe_cache_size",
+            extra.get("expert_cache_pages"),
+        )
+    moe_cache_size = (
+        0
+        if not checkpoint.is_moe
+        else (
+            None
+            if moe_cache_size_raw is None
+            else _checked_int(
+                "moe_cache_size", moe_cache_size_raw, 1, 11_264 if resident else 10_240
+            )
+        )
+    )
+    moe_cache_auto = (
+        False
+        if not checkpoint.is_moe
+        else extra.get("moe_cache_auto", moe_cache_size is None)
+    )
+    if not isinstance(moe_cache_auto, bool):
+        raise ValueError("moe_cache_auto must be a boolean")
+    if moe_cache_auto and moe_cache_size is not None:
+        raise ValueError("moe_cache_auto cannot be combined with a fixed cache size")
+    if not moe_cache_auto and moe_cache_size is None:
+        raise ValueError("moe_cache_auto=false requires a fixed cache size")
+    kv_cache_tokens_raw = extra.get(
+        "kv_cache_tokens",
+        _DEFAULT_DENSE_KV_CACHE_TOKENS if not checkpoint.is_moe else None,
+    )
+    if (
+        checkpoint.is_moe
+        and checkpoint.quant_format in {"nvfp4", "q2_0"}
+        and not has_explicit_cache_size
+        and not has_explicit_cache_auto
+        and kv_cache_tokens_raw is None
+    ):
+        kv_cache_tokens_raw = _DEFAULT_NVFP4_KV_CACHE_TOKENS
+    kv_cache_tokens = (
+        None
+        if kv_cache_tokens_raw is None
+        else _checked_int(
+            "kv_cache_tokens",
+            kv_cache_tokens_raw,
+            _MIN_KV_CACHE_TOKENS,
+            1_048_576,
+        )
+    )
+    if checkpoint.is_moe and moe_cache_auto and kv_cache_tokens is not None:
+        raise ValueError("kv_cache_tokens cannot be combined with moe_cache_auto=true")
+    if resident:
+        # 固定池由常驻区、动态区和两个512槽预填充缓冲区组成，预算按实际几何检查。
+        kv_cache_tokens = 8192 if kv_cache_tokens is None else kv_cache_tokens
+        required_cache = resident + dynamic + 1024
+        if (
+            moe_cache_size != required_cache
+            or moe_cache_auto
+            or kv_cache_tokens != 8192
+            or cuda_graph_max_bs > 1
+            or max_prefill_length > 4096
+            or ple_backend != "disk"
+            or memory_ratio < 0.9
+        ):
+            raise ValueError(
+                f"Protected Q2 requires cache{required_cache}/KV8192, graph<=1, prefill<=4096, disk PLE and memory_ratio>=0.9"
+            )
+    # 1024 分块已通过真实 verify、事务提交与状态扩缩容；分块大小不再绑定旧4096预算。
+    if mtp_enabled and (cache_type != "radix" or cuda_graph_max_bs != 1):
+        raise ValueError("MTP requires radix cache and graph1")
+    _validate_legacy_noop_options(extra)
+    vram_bytes, host_bytes = _runtime_budgets(
+        checkpoint,
+        extra,
+        resident=resident,
+        dynamic=dynamic,
+        mtp_enabled=mtp_enabled,
+        ple_backend=ple_backend,
+    )
+    return _RuntimeOptions(
+        gpu_index=int(match.group(1)),
+        quant_format=checkpoint.quant_format,
+        kernel_cache=kernel_cache,
+        memory_ratio=memory_ratio,
+        attention_backend=str(attention_backend),
+        ple_backend=str(ple_backend),
+        cache_type=str(cache_type),
+        max_seq_len=max_seq_len,
+        max_output_tokens=max_output_tokens,
+        max_prefill_length=max_prefill_length,
+        kv_reserve_tokens=kv_reserve_tokens,
+        kv_cache_tokens=kv_cache_tokens,
+        cuda_graph_max_bs=cuda_graph_max_bs,
+        decode_log_interval=decode_log_interval,
+        moe_backend="offload" if checkpoint.is_moe else "fused",
+        expert_load=expert_load,
+        nvfp4_backend=str(nvfp4_backend),
+        moe_cache_size=moe_cache_size,
+        moe_cache_auto=moe_cache_auto,
+        moe_q2_resident_experts=resident,
+        moe_q2_dynamic_slots=dynamic,
+        startup_timeout_s=_float_option(
+            extra,
+            "startup_timeout_seconds",
+            600.0 if flash_next else 180.0,
+            1.0,
+            1800.0,
+        ),
+        request_timeout_s=_float_option(
+            extra,
+            "request_deadline_seconds",
+            DEFAULT_REQUEST_DEADLINE_SECONDS,
+            1.0,
+            14_400.0,
+        ),
+        cancel_settle_timeout_s=_float_option(
+            extra, "cancel_settle_timeout_seconds", 10.0, 0.1, 60.0
+        ),
+        shutdown_timeout_s=_float_option(
+            extra, "shutdown_timeout_seconds", 15.0, 0.1, 60.0
+        ),
+        vram_estimate_bytes=vram_bytes,
+        host_commit_estimate_bytes=host_bytes,
+        mtp_runtime_path=mtp_root,
+        mtp_manifest_sha256=mtp_digest,
+        mtp_compact_state=compact_state,
+        mtp_draft_policy=draft_policy,
+        linear_state_cache_slots=state_cache_slots,
+        moe_q2_adapt_every=adapt_every,
+        moe_q2_adapt_decay=adapt_decay,
+        gguf_embedding_host=embedding_host,
+        ple_row_cache_rows=row_cache_rows,
+        moe_q2_prefill_workspace=workspace,
+        fixed_q2_rebuild_reserve_mib=rebuild_reserve,
+    )
+
+
+def _runtime_budgets(checkpoint, extra, *, resident, dynamic, mtp_enabled, ple_backend):
+    """只解析一次预算；自定义驻留几何必须覆盖真实压缩专家的GPU和host所有权。"""
+    identity = (checkpoint.profile, checkpoint.quant_format)
+    if resident:
+        gpu_default = (
+            22 if mtp_enabled else (21 if resident == 7168 else 23)
+        ) * 1024**3
+        host_default = (27 if resident == 7168 else 25) * 1024**3
+    else:
+        gpu_default, host_default = (
+            _DEFAULT_VRAM_BYTES[identity],
+            _DEFAULT_HOST_BYTES[identity],
+        )
+    if checkpoint.profile == "qwen3.8-flash-next" and ple_backend == "pinned":
+        host_default += 48 * 1024**3
+    allowed_resident = {7168} if mtp_enabled else {7168, 9216}
+    custom_capacity = resident and (resident not in allowed_resident or dynamic != 1024)
+    if (
+        custom_capacity
+        and not {"vram_estimate_bytes", "host_commit_estimate_bytes"} <= extra.keys()
+    ):
+        raise ValueError(
+            "Custom protected Q2 capacity requires explicit GPU and host budgets"
+        )
+    gpu = _int_option(extra, "vram_estimate_bytes", gpu_default, 1, 128 * 1024**3)
+    host = _int_option(
+        extra, "host_commit_estimate_bytes", host_default, 1, 1024 * 1024**3
+    )
+    if custom_capacity:
+        # 原两档绑定24GiB验收预算；其他几何不能继承旧机器的容量假设。
+        geometry = checkpoint.expert_geometry
+        per_expert = geometry["expert_bytes"] // (
+            geometry["layers"] * geometry["experts_per_layer"]
+        )
+        if gpu < (resident + dynamic + 1024) * per_expert:
+            raise ValueError(
+                "vram_estimate_bytes is smaller than its compressed expert ownership bytes"
+            )
+        if host < geometry["expert_bytes"] - resident * per_expert:
+            raise ValueError(
+                "host_commit_estimate_bytes is smaller than its compressed expert ownership bytes"
+            )
+    return gpu, host
+
+
+def _worker_command() -> list[str]:
+    """从当前发布物启动隔离 worker，同时支持源码树与单文件原生包。"""
+
+    import silitok_speed
+
+    package_parent = str(Path(silitok_speed.__spec__.origin).resolve().parent.parent)
+    runtime_dir = get_config().freetoken_runtime_dir
+    runtime_root = str(Path(runtime_dir).expanduser().resolve()) if runtime_dir else ""
+    return [
+        sys.executable,
+        "-I",
+        "-B",
+        "-X",
+        "utf8",
+        "-u",
+        "-c",
+        "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv.pop(1)); "
+        "runtime=sys.argv.pop(1); sys.path.insert(0, runtime) if runtime else None; "
+        "from silitok_speed.runtimes.freetoken.worker import main; "
+        "raise SystemExit(main())",
+        package_parent,
+        runtime_root,
+    ]
+
+
+def _validate_inputs(inputs: Any) -> tuple[list[dict[str, str]], bool]:
+    """只接收 FreeToken Qwen3.x 已冻结的纯文本消息形态。"""
+
+    if not isinstance(inputs, dict):
+        raise BackendInputError("Qwen3.x inputs must be an object")
+    for field in ("tools", "tool_choice"):
+        if inputs.get(field):
+            raise BackendInputError(
+                f"FreeToken Qwen3.x does not support {field}",
+                code="unsupported_feature",
+            )
+    enable_thinking = inputs.get("enable_thinking", False)
+    if not isinstance(enable_thinking, bool):
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "enable_thinking must be a boolean",
+            code="invalid_request",
+        )
+    messages = inputs.get("messages")
+    if not isinstance(messages, list) or not messages:
+        raise BackendInputError("Qwen3.x requires non-empty messages")
+    normalized: list[dict[str, str]] = []
+    for index, message in enumerate(messages):
+        if not isinstance(message, dict):
+            raise BackendInputError(f"messages[{index}] must be an object")
+        role = message.get("role")
+        content = message.get("content")
+        if role not in {"system", "user", "assistant"}:
+            raise BackendInputError(
+                f"messages[{index}].role is unsupported",
+                code="unsupported_feature",
+            )
+        if not isinstance(content, str):
+            raise BackendInputError(
+                f"messages[{index}].content must be a text string",
+                code="unsupported_feature",
+            )
+        if message.get("tool_calls") or message.get("reasoning_content") is not None:
+            raise BackendInputError(
+                f"messages[{index}] contains deferred structured content",
+                code="unsupported_feature",
+            )
+        normalized.append({"role": role, "content": content})
+    return normalized, enable_thinking
+
+
+def _sampling_values(
+    checkpoint: FreeTokenCheckpoint,
+    *,
+    enable_thinking: bool,
+    values: dict[str, Any],
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """把公共 chat 参数投影成 FreeToken SamplingParams 的稳定子集。"""
+
+    for name in ("presence_penalty", "seed"):
+        if name in values:
+            raise BackendInputError(
+                f"FreeToken Qwen3.x does not support {name}",
+                code="unsupported_feature",
+                param=name,
+            )
+    repetition_penalty = _request_float(
+        values,
+        "repetition_penalty",
+        1.0,
+    )
+    if repetition_penalty != 1.0:
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "FreeToken Qwen3.x does not support repetition_penalty",
+            code="unsupported_feature",
+        )
+    no_repeat_ngram_size = values.get("no_repeat_ngram_size", 0)
+    if isinstance(no_repeat_ngram_size, bool) or not isinstance(
+        no_repeat_ngram_size, int
+    ):
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "no_repeat_ngram_size must be an integer",
+            code="invalid_request",
+            param="no_repeat_ngram_size",
+        )
+    if no_repeat_ngram_size != 0:
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "FreeToken Qwen3.x does not support no_repeat_ngram_size",
+            code="unsupported_feature",
+        )
+    default_temperature = (
+        float(checkpoint.generation_defaults["temperature"]) if enable_thinking else 0.7
+    )
+    default_top_p = (
+        float(checkpoint.generation_defaults["top_p"]) if enable_thinking else 0.8
+    )
+    temperature = _request_float(
+        values,
+        "temperature",
+        default_temperature,
+    )
+    top_p = _request_float(values, "top_p", default_top_p)
+    top_k = values.get("top_k", checkpoint.generation_defaults["top_k"])
+    do_sample = values.get("do_sample", True)
+    if not isinstance(do_sample, bool):
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "do_sample must be a boolean",
+            code="invalid_request",
+            param="do_sample",
+        )
+    if not do_sample:
+        temperature = 0.0
+    if not 0.0 <= temperature <= 2.0:
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "temperature is outside [0, 2]",
+            code="invalid_request",
+        )
+    if not 0.0 <= top_p <= 1.0:
+        raise BackendInputError("top_p is outside [0, 1]", code="invalid_request")
+    if isinstance(top_k, bool) or not isinstance(top_k, int) or top_k < 0:
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "top_k must be an integer >= 0",
+            code="invalid_request",
+        )
+    max_tokens = _normalize_output_budget(
+        values.get("max_tokens", values.get("max_new_tokens", 512))
+    )
+    return {
+        "temperature": temperature,
+        "top_p": top_p,
+        "top_k": top_k,
+        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
+        "stop": list(_normalize_stop(values.get("stop"))),
+    }
+
+
+def _normalize_output_budget(value: Any) -> int:
+    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value <= 0:
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "max_tokens must be a positive integer",
+            code="invalid_parameter",
+            param="max_tokens",
+        )
+    return value
+
+
+def _request_float(
+    values: dict[str, Any],
+    name: str,
+    default: float,
+) -> float:
+    value = values.get(name, default)
+    if (
+        isinstance(value, bool)
+        or not isinstance(value, (int, float))
+        or not math.isfinite(float(value))
+    ):
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            f"{name} must be a finite number",
+            code="invalid_request",
+            param=name,
+        )
+    return float(value)
+
+
+def _normalize_stop(value: Any) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+    if value is None:
+        return ()
+    values = (value,) if isinstance(value, str) else value
+    if (
+        not isinstance(values, (list, tuple))
+        or not 1 <= len(values) <= 4
+        or any(
+            not isinstance(item, str) or not item or len(item) > 1024 for item in values
+        )
+    ):
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "stop must contain 1 to 4 non-empty strings of at most 1024 chars",
+            code="invalid_request",
+        )
+    return tuple(values)
+
+
+def _load_tokenizer(
+    checkpoint: FreeTokenCheckpoint,
+    cancel_event: object | None,
+) -> Any:
+    """共享一次本地 tokenizer 解析，等待期间保持 admission 可取消。"""
+
+    root = checkpoint.root
+    key = (root, checkpoint.tokenizer_fingerprint)
+    with _TOKENIZER_CACHE_LOCK:
+        tokenizer = _TOKENIZER_CACHE.get(key)
+        if tokenizer is not None:
+            return tokenizer
+        load = _TOKENIZER_LOADS.get(key)
+        if load is None:
+            load = Future()
+            _TOKENIZER_LOADS[key] = load
+            threading.Thread(
+                target=_load_tokenizer_worker,
+                args=(key, load),
+                name=f"freetoken-qwen-tokenizer-{root.name}",
+                daemon=True,
+            ).start()
+    while True:
+        _check_cancel_event(cancel_event)
+        try:
+            return load.result(timeout=0.05)
+        except FutureTimeoutError:
+            continue
+
+
+def _load_tokenizer_worker(key: _TokenizerCacheKey, load: Future[Any]) -> None:
+    root, _fingerprint = key
+    try:
+        from transformers import AutoTokenizer
+
+        tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
+            root,
+            local_files_only=True,
+            trust_remote_code=False,
+        )
+        if not tokenizer.chat_template:
+            raise ValueError("Qwen3.x tokenizer has no chat_template")
+    except Exception as exc:
+        with _TOKENIZER_CACHE_LOCK:
+            if _TOKENIZER_LOADS.get(key) is load:
+                del _TOKENIZER_LOADS[key]
+        load.set_exception(ModelCorruptError("Failed to load the Qwen3.x tokenizer"))
+        logger.debug("Qwen3.x tokenizer load failed", exc_info=exc)
+        return
+    with _TOKENIZER_CACHE_LOCK:
+        _TOKENIZER_CACHE[key] = tokenizer
+        if _TOKENIZER_LOADS.get(key) is load:
+            del _TOKENIZER_LOADS[key]
+    load.set_result(tokenizer)
+
+
+def _apply_chat_template(
+    tokenizer: Any,
+    messages: list[dict[str, str]],
+    *,
+    enable_thinking: bool,
+) -> list[int]:
+    try:
+        encoded = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
+            messages,
+            add_generation_prompt=True,
+            tokenize=True,
+            enable_thinking=enable_thinking,
+            return_dict=False,
+        )
+    except Exception as exc:
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "Qwen3.x chat template rejected the messages",
+            code="invalid_request",
+        ) from exc
+    if hasattr(encoded, "tolist"):
+        encoded = encoded.tolist()
+    if encoded and isinstance(encoded[0], list):
+        if len(encoded) != 1:
+            raise ModelCorruptError("Qwen3.x tokenizer returned a batch")
+        encoded = encoded[0]
+    if not isinstance(encoded, list) or any(
+        isinstance(token, bool) or not isinstance(token, int) for token in encoded
+    ):
+        raise ModelCorruptError("Qwen3.x tokenizer returned invalid token ids")
+    return encoded
+
+
+def _validate_total_budget(prompt_tokens: int, output_tokens: int) -> None:
+    if output_tokens > MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS:
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            f"max_tokens exceeds the Qwen3.x output limit {MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS}",
+            code="max_output_tokens_exceeded",
+            param="max_tokens",
+        )
+    if prompt_tokens + output_tokens > MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS:
+        raise BackendInputError(
+            "prompt plus output exceeds the Qwen3.x context window",
+            code="context_length_exceeded",
+            param="max_tokens",
+        )
+
+
+def _require_request_message(message: dict[str, Any], request_id: str) -> None:
+    if message.get("request_id") != request_id:
+        raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken worker response request_id does not match")
+
+
+def _text_field(message: dict[str, Any], name: str) -> str:
+    value = message.get(name)
+    if not isinstance(value, str):
+        raise WireProtocolError(f"FreeToken worker field {name!r} is not text")
+    return value
+
+
+def _non_negative_int(message: dict[str, Any], name: str) -> int:
+    value = message.get(name)
+    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value < 0:
+        raise WireProtocolError(
+            f"FreeToken worker field {name!r} is not a non-negative integer"
+        )
+    return value
+
+
+def _bool_field(message: dict[str, Any], name: str) -> bool:
+    value = message.get(name)
+    if not isinstance(value, bool):
+        raise WireProtocolError(f"FreeToken worker field {name!r} is not a boolean")
+    return value
+
+
+def _final_projection(
+    message: dict[str, Any],
+    tokens: list[int],
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """验证 terminal frame，不把 worker 任意对象直接暴露给 Pipeline。"""
+
+    text = _text_field(message, "text")
+    reasoning = message.get("reasoning_content")
+    if reasoning is not None and not isinstance(reasoning, str):
+        raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken reasoning_content is invalid")
+    finish_reason = message.get("finish_reason")
+    if not isinstance(finish_reason, str) or not finish_reason:
+        raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken finish_reason is invalid")
+    usage = message.get("usage")
+    if not isinstance(usage, dict):
+        raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken usage is invalid")
+    input_tokens = _non_negative_int(usage, "input_tokens")
+    output_tokens = _non_negative_int(usage, "output_tokens")
+    if output_tokens != len(tokens):
+        raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken output token accounting does not match")
+    return {
+        "type": "final",
+        "reasoning_content": reasoning,
+        "text": text,
+        "tokens": list(tokens),
+        "finish_reason": finish_reason,
+        "usage": {
+            "input_tokens": input_tokens,
+            "output_tokens": output_tokens,
+        },
+    }
+
+
+def _request_error_message(message: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
+    code = message.get("code")
+    if code == "context_length_exceeded":
+        return "Qwen3.x prompt exceeds the configured context window"
+    return "FreeToken rejected the Qwen3.x request"
+
+
+def _validate_legacy_noop_options(extra: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
+    """保留旧 native manifest 可读性，但不伪装成 FreeToken 调优项。
+
+    FreeToken 自己管理 expert staging 与 prefill 分块，没有与旧 Speed
+    native runtime 等价的旋钮。这两个字段仅为已部署 manifest 的解析兼容
+    而保留：值仍严格校验，但不会进入 worker 启动协议。
+    """
+
+    limits = {
+        "expert_staging_pages": (1, 256),
+        "prefill_chunk_size": (1, 2048),
+    }
+    for name, (minimum, maximum) in limits.items():
+        if name in extra:
+            _checked_int(name, extra[name], minimum, maximum)
+
+
+def _process_tree_contains_pid(
+    process: subprocess.Popen[bytes],
+    runtime_pid: int,
+) -> bool:
+    """Windows venv launcher 可代理真实解释器，但握手 PID 必须属于其进程树。"""
+
+    if runtime_pid == process.pid:
+        return process.poll() is None
+    try:
+        parent = psutil.Process(process.pid)
+        return any(
+            child.pid == runtime_pid for child in parent.children(recursive=True)
+        )
+    except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
+        return False
+
+
+def _worker_cuda_visibility(logical_index: int, inherited: str | None) -> str:
+    """把父进程逻辑 CUDA 序号收敛为 worker 唯一可见的同一块设备。"""
+
+    if inherited is None:
+        return str(logical_index)
+    visible = [item.strip() for item in inherited.split(",")]
+    if any(not item or item == "-1" for item in visible) or logical_index >= len(
+        visible
+    ):
+        raise ValueError(
+            "FreeToken Qwen3.x device is outside the parent CUDA visibility"
+        )
+    return visible[logical_index]
+
+
+def _terminate_owned_process(
+    process: subprocess.Popen[bytes],
+    *,
+    timeout_s: float,
+) -> None:
+    """回收启动时记录的进程及后代；全部等待共享一个总期限。"""
+
+    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, timeout_s)
+    try:
+        parent = psutil.Process(process.pid)
+        descendants = parent.children(recursive=True)
+    except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
+        parent = None
+        descendants = []
+
+    # 收到 worker 的 stopped 回执后，先给解释器一个短窗口自然退出；
+    # Windows venv launcher 否则会在子进程刚清理完 CUDA 时立刻收到 BREAK。
+    if process.poll() is None:
+        try:
+            process.wait(timeout=min(0.25, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
+        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+            pass
+
+    if process.poll() is None:
+        try:
+            if os.name == "nt" and hasattr(signal, "CTRL_BREAK_EVENT"):
+                process.send_signal(signal.CTRL_BREAK_EVENT)
+            elif os.name != "nt":
+                os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
+            else:
+                process.terminate()
+            process.wait(timeout=min(1.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
+        except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
+            pass
+
+    alive = []
+    for item in [*descendants, *([parent] if parent is not None else [])]:
+        try:
+            if item.is_running():
+                item.terminate()
+                alive.append(item)
+        except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
+            continue
+    if alive:
+        try:
+            _, alive = psutil.wait_procs(
+                alive,
+                timeout=min(1.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())),
+            )
+        except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
+            pass
+    for item in alive:
+        try:
+            item.kill()
+        except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
+            continue
+    if alive:
+        try:
+            _, alive = psutil.wait_procs(
+                alive,
+                timeout=max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()),
+            )
+        except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
+            pass
+    if process.poll() is None:
+        try:
+            process.kill()
+        except OSError:
+            pass
+    try:
+        process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
+    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
+        logger.error("FreeToken worker did not exit before its cleanup deadline")
+    for item in alive:
+        try:
+            if item.is_running():
+                logger.error(
+                    "FreeToken worker descendant %s survived its cleanup deadline",
+                    item.pid,
+                )
+        except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
+            continue
+
+
+def _check_cancel_event(cancel_event: object | None) -> None:
+    if cancel_event is not None and bool(cancel_event.is_set()):
+        _raise_cancelled()
+
+
+def _check_cancel_callback(check: Callable[[], bool] | None) -> None:
+    if check is not None and bool(check()):
+        _raise_cancelled()
+
+
+def _checked_int(name: str, value: Any, minimum: int, maximum: int) -> int:
+    if (
+        isinstance(value, bool)
+        or not isinstance(value, int)
+        or not minimum <= value <= maximum
+    ):
+        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be an integer in [{minimum}, {maximum}]")
+    return value
+
+
+def _int_option(
+    extra: dict[str, Any],
+    name: str,
+    default: int,
+    minimum: int,
+    maximum: int,
+) -> int:
+    return _checked_int(name, extra.get(name, default), minimum, maximum)
+
+
+def _bool_option(extra: dict[str, Any], name: str) -> bool:
+    """在公开配置入口保持严格布尔语义，避免字符串false被当成启用。"""
+    value = extra.get(name, False)
+    if not isinstance(value, bool):
+        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a boolean")
+    return value
+
+
+def _float_option(
+    extra: dict[str, Any],
+    name: str,
+    default: float,
+    minimum: float,
+    maximum: float,
+) -> float:
+    value = extra.get(name, default)
+    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
+        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a number in [{minimum}, {maximum}]")
+    result = float(value)
+    if not minimum <= result <= maximum:
+        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a number in [{minimum}, {maximum}]")
+    return result
+
+
+FreeTokenQwen36Backend = FreeTokenBackend
+Qwen36Backend = FreeTokenBackend
+FreeTokenQwen38Backend = FreeTokenBackend
+
+__all__ = [
+    "FreeTokenBackend",
+    "FreeTokenQwen36Backend",
+    "FreeTokenQwen38Backend",
+    "Qwen36Backend",
+]

-def _runtime_budgets(checkpoint, extra, *, resident, dynamic, mtp_enabled, ple_backend):

-        per_expert = geometry['expert_bytes'] // (geometry['layers'] * geometry['experts_per_layer'])

+"""FastAPI-free chat request normalization shared by every entry path."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from typing import Any
+import math
+
+
+MAX_CHAT_COMPLETION_TOKENS = 131_072
+DEFAULT_MODEL_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS = 16_384
+
+
+class ChatContractError(ValueError):
+    """携带稳定机器字段的聊天请求合同错误。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, message: str, *, code: str, param: str) -> None:
+        super().__init__(message)
+        self.code = code
+        self.param = param
+
+
+def normalize_completion_token_budget(
+    values: Mapping[str, Any],
+) -> tuple[int | None, str | None]:
+    """把两个 OpenAI 输出预算别名归一为一个正整数。"""
+
+    max_tokens = values.get("max_tokens")
+    max_completion_tokens = values.get("max_completion_tokens")
+    if max_tokens is not None and max_completion_tokens is not None:
+        raise ChatContractError(
+            "Specify only one of max_tokens and max_completion_tokens.",
+            code="conflicting_parameters",
+            param="max_completion_tokens",
+        )
+    param = "max_tokens" if max_tokens is not None else "max_completion_tokens"
+    budget = max_tokens if max_tokens is not None else max_completion_tokens
+    if budget is None:
+        return None, None
+    if isinstance(budget, bool) or not isinstance(budget, int):
+        raise ChatContractError(
+            f"{param} must be an integer.",
+            code="invalid_parameter",
+            param=param,
+        )
+    if budget <= 0 or budget > MAX_CHAT_COMPLETION_TOKENS:
+        raise ChatContractError(
+            f"{param} must be between 1 and {MAX_CHAT_COMPLETION_TOKENS}.",
+            code="invalid_parameter",
+            param=param,
+        )
+    return budget, param
+
+
+_EXTENDED_REQUEST_FIELDS = {
+    "frequency_penalty",
+    "logprobs",
+    "min_p",
+    "presence_penalty",
+    "preserve_thinking",
+    "response_format",
+    "seed",
+    "stop",
+    "top_k",
+    "top_logprobs",
+}
+
+
+def validate_model_fields(values: dict, capability: dict | None) -> None:
+    """按 discovery catalog 拒绝模型首版明确延期的请求能力。"""
+    messages = values.get("messages")
+    if (
+        not isinstance(messages, list)
+        or not messages
+        or any((not isinstance(message, dict) for message in messages))
+    ):
+        raise ChatContractError(
+            "messages must be a non-empty list of objects.",
+            code="invalid_request",
+            param="messages",
+        )
+    for index, message in enumerate(messages):
+        if not isinstance(message.get("role"), str):
+            raise ChatContractError(
+                f"messages[{index}].role must be a string.",
+                code="invalid_request",
+                param=f"messages.{index}.role",
+            )
+        content = message.get("content")
+        if content is not None and not isinstance(content, (str, list)):
+            raise ChatContractError(
+                f"messages[{index}].content must be text, an array, or null.",
+                code="invalid_request",
+                param=f"messages.{index}.content",
+            )
+        if isinstance(content, list) and any(
+            not isinstance(part, dict)
+            or (part.get("text") is not None and not isinstance(part["text"], str))
+            for part in content
+        ):
+            raise ChatContractError(
+                f"messages[{index}].content contains invalid content parts.",
+                code="invalid_request",
+                param=f"messages.{index}.content",
+            )
+    if capability is None:
+        return
+    has_supported_contract = "supported_request_fields" in capability
+    supported = set(capability.get("supported_request_fields") or [])
+    unsupported = set(capability.get("unsupported_request_fields") or [])
+    if has_supported_contract:
+        unsupported.update(_EXTENDED_REQUEST_FIELDS - supported)
+    supplied = {
+        "stop": values.get("stop"),
+        "response_format": values.get("response_format"),
+        "logprobs": values.get("logprobs"),
+        "top_logprobs": values.get("top_logprobs"),
+        "tool_choice": values.get("tool_choice"),
+        "frequency_penalty": values.get("frequency_penalty"),
+        "min_p": values.get("min_p"),
+        "preserve_thinking": values.get("preserve_thinking"),
+        "top_k": values.get("top_k"),
+        "presence_penalty": values.get("presence_penalty"),
+        "seed": values.get("seed"),
+        "tools": values.get("tools"),
+        "parallel_tool_calls": values.get("parallel_tool_calls"),
+    }
+    for field, value in supplied.items():
+        if (
+            field in unsupported
+            and value is not None
+            and (not (field == "tools" and value == []))
+        ):
+            raise ChatContractError(
+                f"Model {values.get('model')!r} does not support request field {field!r} in its current release.",
+                code="unsupported_feature",
+                param=field,
+            )
+    if has_supported_contract:
+        for field in supported & _EXTENDED_REQUEST_FIELDS:
+            value = supplied.get(field)
+            if value is not None:
+                _validate_model_deferred_field(values.get("model"), field, value)
+    if values.get("parallel_tool_calls") is not None and (
+        not capability.get("supports_tool_calls", False)
+    ):
+        raise ChatContractError(
+            f"Model {values.get('model')!r} does not support parallel_tool_calls.",
+            code="unsupported_feature",
+            param="parallel_tool_calls",
+        )
+    if values.get("enable_thinking") is True and (
+        not capability.get("supports_thinking", True)
+    ):
+        raise ChatContractError(
+            f"Model {values.get('model')!r} does not expose thinking output.",
+            code="unsupported_feature",
+            param="enable_thinking",
+        )
+    if not capability.get("supports_multimodal", True):
+        for message in values.get("messages"):
+            if isinstance(message.get("content"), list):
+                raise ChatContractError(
+                    f"Model {values.get('model')!r} accepts text strings only.",
+                    code="unsupported_feature",
+                    param="messages.content",
+                )
+    for message in values.get("messages"):
+        if message.get("reasoning_content") is not None and (
+            not capability.get(
+                "supports_thinking_history", capability.get("supports_thinking", True)
+            )
+        ):
+            raise ChatContractError(
+                f"Model {values.get('model')!r} does not preserve structured reasoning history.",
+                code="unsupported_feature",
+                param="messages.reasoning_content",
+            )
+        if not capability.get("supports_tool_calls", False) and (
+            message.get("role") == "tool" or message.get("tool_calls")
+        ):
+            raise ChatContractError(
+                f"Model {values.get('model')!r} does not support tool history.",
+                code="tool_calls_not_supported",
+                param="messages.tool_calls",
+            )
+
+
+def _invalid_deferred_field(model_id: str, field: str, message: str) -> None:
+    raise ChatContractError(
+        f"Model {model_id!r} request field {field!r} {message}.",
+        code="invalid_request",
+        param=field,
+    )
+
+
+def _validate_model_deferred_field(model_id: str, field: str, value: Any) -> None:
+    """只在 backend 明确声明支持后校验其模型专属字段。"""
+    if field == "top_k":
+        if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value < 0:
+            _invalid_deferred_field(model_id, field, "must be an integer >= 0")
+    elif field in {"presence_penalty", "frequency_penalty"}:
+        if (
+            isinstance(value, bool)
+            or not isinstance(value, (int, float))
+            or (not math.isfinite(float(value)))
+            or (not -2.0 <= float(value) <= 2.0)
+        ):
+            _invalid_deferred_field(
+                model_id, field, "must be finite and within [-2, 2]"
+            )
+    elif field == "min_p":
+        if (
+            isinstance(value, bool)
+            or not isinstance(value, (int, float))
+            or (not math.isfinite(float(value)))
+            or (not 0.0 <= float(value) <= 1.0)
+        ):
+            _invalid_deferred_field(model_id, field, "must be finite and within [0, 1]")
+    elif field == "seed":
+        if (
+            isinstance(value, bool)
+            or not isinstance(value, int)
+            or (not -(2**63) <= value <= 2**63 - 1)
+        ):
+            _invalid_deferred_field(model_id, field, "must be a signed 64-bit integer")
+    elif field == "stop":
+        values = [value] if isinstance(value, str) else value
+        if not isinstance(values, list) or not 1 <= len(values) <= 4:
+            _invalid_deferred_field(model_id, field, "must contain 1 to 4 strings")
+        if any(
+            (
+                not isinstance(item, str) or not item or len(item) > 1024
+                for item in values
+            )
+        ):
+            _invalid_deferred_field(
+                model_id, field, "strings must be non-empty and at most 1024 characters"
+            )

-def _expert_storage(engine) -> dict[str, Any]:
-    """汇总实际 host bank 字节与锁页状态，用于全量压缩专家的验收回执。"""
-    cache = getattr(engine, "moe_offload_cache", None)
-    if cache is None:
-        return {}
-    tensors = [tensor for per_layer in cache.bank_sources.values() for tensor in per_layer]
-    ownership = getattr(cache, 'protected_payload', None)
-    result = {
-        "format": cache.quant_format,
-        "layers": cache.num_layers,
-        "experts_per_layer": cache.num_experts,
-        "host_bytes": sum(tensor.nbytes for tensor in tensors),
-        "all_pinned": all(tensor.is_pinned() for tensor in tensors if tensor.numel()),
-        "gpu_cache_bytes": sum(tensor.nbytes for tensor in cache.bank_caches.values()),
-    }
-    if ownership is not None:
-        result.update(protected_experts=len(ownership.protected_slots), dynamic_slots=ownership.dynamic,
-            protected_gpu_bytes=ownership.gpu_bank_bytes // ownership.slots * len(ownership.protected_slots))
-    return result

+"""FreeToken Qwen3.x checkpoint 与公共能力合同。
+
+本模块只读取小型 JSON 元数据，不导入 Torch 或 FreeToken。discovery、
+preflight 与 worker 启动因此能在打开大权重前共享同一个拒绝边界。
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import re
+import struct
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from functools import lru_cache
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Literal, Mapping
+
+
+QuantFormat = Literal["fp8", "nvfp4", "q2_0"]
+ModelProfile = Literal["qwen3.6-35b-a3b", "qwen3.8-27b", "qwen3.8-flash-next"]
+
+RELEASE = json.loads(
+    (Path(__file__).parents[2] / "qwen36" / "release.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+)
+FREETOKEN_VERSION = RELEASE["version"]
+FREETOKEN_WHEEL_FILENAME = Path(RELEASE["wheel"]["path"]).name
+FREETOKEN_WHEEL_SHA256 = RELEASE["wheel"]["sha256"]
+TORCH_VERSION = RELEASE["torch"]
+TORCH_CUDA_VERSION = RELEASE["cuda"]
+PYTHON_MINOR = tuple(RELEASE["python_minor"])
+CUDA_CAPABILITY = (int(RELEASE["sm"]) // 10, int(RELEASE["sm"]) % 10)
+MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS = 256 * 1024
+MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS = 4096
+DEFAULT_REQUEST_DEADLINE_SECONDS = 1800.0
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class _ProfileSpec:
+    name: ModelProfile
+    label: str
+    architecture: str
+    model_type: str
+    text_values: Mapping[str, Any]
+    layer_types: tuple[str, ...]
+    is_moe: bool
+
+
+def _hybrid_layer_types(count: int) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+    return tuple(
+        "full_attention" if index % 4 == 3 else "linear_attention"
+        for index in range(count)
+    )
+

+    {

+        "chat_template.jinja",
