+class FreeTokenCheckpointError(ValueError):
+    """Checkpoint 元数据不属于受支持的 Qwen3.x 布局。"""
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class FreeTokenCheckpoint:
+    """worker 打开权重前使用的小型不可变清单。"""
+
+    root: Path
+    profile: ModelProfile
+    profile_label: str
+    is_moe: bool
+    quant_format: QuantFormat
+    shard_names: tuple[str, ...]
+    shard_bytes: int
+    tokenizer_fingerprint: tuple[tuple[str, int, int, int], ...]
+    generation_defaults: Mapping[str, float | int | tuple[int, ...]]
+    expert_geometry: Mapping[str, int] | None = None
+    weight_layout: str = "hf"
+
+
+def inspect_freetoken_checkpoint(
+    weights_path: str | Path,
+    declared_quantization: str | None = None,
+) -> FreeTokenCheckpoint:
+    """校验量化元数据、完整来源闭包、tokenizer 与采样默认值。"""
+
+    try:
+        root = Path(weights_path).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "Qwen3.x checkpoint directory does not exist"
+        ) from exc
+    if not root.is_dir():
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("Qwen3.x checkpoint must be a directory")
+    checkpoint = _inspect_cached(str(root), _checkpoint_fingerprint(root))
+    if declared_quantization is not None:
+        if not isinstance(declared_quantization, str):
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("manifest quantization must be a string")
+        declared = declared_quantization.strip().lower()
+        aliases = (
+            {"q2_0", "q2_0_strata"}
+            if checkpoint.weight_layout == "gguf_strata"
+            else _QUANTIZATION_ALIASES[checkpoint.quant_format]
+        )
+        if declared not in aliases:
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                "manifest quantization does not match checkpoint format: "
+                f"declared {declared_quantization!r}, "
+                f"detected {checkpoint.quant_format!r}"
+            )
+    return checkpoint
+
+
+@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
+def _inspect_cached(
+    root_text: str,
+    fingerprint: tuple[tuple[str, int, int, int], ...],
+) -> FreeTokenCheckpoint:
+    root = Path(root_text)
+    config = _read_object(root / "config.json", "config.json")
+    profile = _profile_for_config(config)
+    _validate_model_shape(config, profile)
+
+    if config.get("freetoken_gguf_source") is not None:
+        return _inspect_strata_gguf_checkpoint(root, config, profile, fingerprint)
+    quant = config.get("quantization_config")
+    if not isinstance(quant, Mapping):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("checkpoint has no quantization_config object")
+    quant_format = _detect_quant_format(quant, profile=profile)
+    expert_geometry = None
+    if config.get("freetoken_expert_source") is not None:
+        if profile.name != "qwen3.8-flash-next" or quant_format != "nvfp4":
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                "Q2_0 external experts require the supported Flash Next HF trunk"
+            )
+        expert_geometry = _validate_q2_expert_source(root, config)
+        quant_format = "q2_0"
+    if (root / "INVALID-EXPERT-ALIASES.json").exists():
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "partial-expert experimental checkpoints are not supported"
+        )
+
+    index = _read_object(
+        root / "model.safetensors.index.json",
+        "model.safetensors.index.json",
+    )
+    weight_map = index.get("weight_map")
+    if not isinstance(weight_map, Mapping) or not weight_map:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("checkpoint index has no non-empty weight_map")
+    if quant_format == "q2_0" and any(".experts." in key for key in weight_map):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "Q2_0 checkpoint must index only its HF non-expert weights"
+        )
+    shard_values = tuple(weight_map.values())
+    if any(not isinstance(name, str) or not name for name in shard_values):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "checkpoint index contains an invalid shard name"
+        )
+    shard_names = tuple(sorted(set(shard_values)))
+    if any(
+        Path(name).name != name or not name.endswith(".safetensors")
+        for name in shard_names
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "checkpoint index contains a non-local safetensors shard name"
+        )
+    missing = [name for name in shard_names if not (root / name).is_file()]
+    if missing:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            f"checkpoint is missing {len(missing)} indexed safetensors shard(s)"
+        )
+    for required in (
+        "tokenizer.json",
+        "tokenizer_config.json",
+        "generation_config.json",
+    ):
+        if not (root / required).is_file():
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(f"checkpoint is missing {required}")
+
+    try:
+        shard_bytes = sum((root / name).stat().st_size for name in shard_names)
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "cannot inspect checkpoint safetensors shards"
+        ) from exc
+    return FreeTokenCheckpoint(
+        root=root,
+        profile=profile.name,
+        profile_label=profile.label,
+        is_moe=profile.is_moe,
+        quant_format=quant_format,
+        shard_names=shard_names,
+        shard_bytes=shard_bytes,
+        tokenizer_fingerprint=tuple(
+            entry for entry in fingerprint if entry[0] in _TOKENIZER_IDENTITY_NAMES
+        ),
+        generation_defaults=_generation_defaults(root, profile),
+        expert_geometry=expert_geometry,
+    )
+
+
+def _checkpoint_fingerprint(root: Path) -> tuple[tuple[str, int, int, int], ...]:
+    """为影响加载判定的目录项生成廉价缓存身份。"""
+
+    metadata_names = {
+        "config.json",
+        "generation_config.json",
+        "model.safetensors.index.json",
+        "INVALID-EXPERT-ALIASES.json",
+        "mixed-source.json",
+        *_TOKENIZER_IDENTITY_NAMES,
+    }
+    try:
+        entries = []
+        for path in root.iterdir():
+            if path.name not in metadata_names and path.suffix != ".safetensors":
+                continue
+            info = path.stat()
+            entries.append((path.name, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ino))
+        config = _read_object(root / "config.json", "config.json")
+        source = config.get("freetoken_expert_source")
+        if isinstance(source, Mapping) and isinstance(source.get("path"), str):
+            path = Path(source["path"])
+            if not path.is_absolute():
+                path = root / path
+            info = path.stat()
+            entries.append(
+                ("<q2-expert-source>", info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ino)
+            )
+        gguf = config.get("freetoken_gguf_source")
+        if isinstance(gguf, Mapping) and isinstance(gguf.get("shards"), list):
+            for shard in gguf["shards"]:
+                if not isinstance(shard, Mapping) or not isinstance(
+                    shard.get("path"), str
+                ):
+                    raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("GGUF shard path is missing")
+                path = Path(shard["path"])
+                info = (path if path.is_absolute() else root / path).stat()
+                entries.append(
+                    (
+                        "<gguf-source:" + shard["path"] + ">",
+                        info.st_size,
+                        info.st_mtime_ns,
+                        info.st_ino,
+                    )
+                )
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("cannot inspect checkpoint directory") from exc
+    return tuple(sorted(entries))
+
+
+_STRATA_GGUF_REPO = "ISTA-DASLab/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-GGUF"
+_STRATA_GGUF_REVISION = "ed59f92082b1e93c0e96d60a8b11aab089b52f09"
+_STRATA_GGUF_SHARDS = {
+    "Q2_0/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-Q2_0-00001-of-00002.gguf": (
+        37623740192,
+        "69820c02ec7d0b45ef2ebb19d6620299db749fe2aded7f39f93c6b88b199b720",
+    ),
+    "Q2_0/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-Q2_0-00002-of-00002.gguf": (
+        28800138432,
+        "316b46f3a2dbd68c900f43136ab9449f9dcc3725dfd8c794847c204bc161e113",
+    ),
+}
+
+
+def _inspect_strata_gguf_checkpoint(root, config, profile, fingerprint):
+    """按完整两片的离线校验身份预检 GSQ-RCO；张量目录与计算格式由 worker 加载时核验。"""
+    source = config["freetoken_gguf_source"]
+    if (
+        profile.name != "qwen3.8-flash-next"
+        or not isinstance(source, Mapping)
+        or source.get("format") != "gguf_strata"
+        or source.get("revision") != _STRATA_GGUF_REVISION
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("unsupported complete Strata Q2 source")
+    shards = source.get("shards")
+    if not isinstance(shards, list) or len(shards) != 2:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("Strata Q2 requires both complete GGUF shards")
+    seen, paths, total = set(), [], 0
+    for shard in shards:
+        if not isinstance(shard, Mapping):
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("GGUF shard receipt is missing")
+        name = shard.get("filename")
+        if not isinstance(name, str) or name not in _STRATA_GGUF_SHARDS or name in seen:
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                "GGUF pair is missing a shard or repeats one"
+            )
+        size, digest = _STRATA_GGUF_SHARDS[name]
+        if (
+            shard.get("repo") != _STRATA_GGUF_REPO
+            or shard.get("revision") != _STRATA_GGUF_REVISION
+            or shard.get("size") != size
+            or shard.get("sha256") != digest
+            or not isinstance(shard.get("path"), str)
+        ):
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                "GGUF shard does not match the certified Q2 identity"
+            )
+        path = Path(shard["path"])
+        path = path if path.is_absolute() else root / path
+        try:
+            info = path.stat()
+            with path.open("rb") as stream:
+                header = stream.read(24)
+        except OSError as exc:
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("GGUF shard is unavailable") from exc
+        if (
+            info.st_size != size
+            or info.st_mtime_ns != shard.get("verified_mtime_ns")
+            or len(header) != 24
+            or header[:4] != b"GGUF"
+            or struct.unpack_from("<I", header, 4)[0] != 3
+        ):
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                "GGUF shard changed after offline checksum verification"
+            )
+        seen.add(name)
+        paths.append(str(path.resolve()))
+        total += size
+    expert = config.get("freetoken_expert_source")
+    first = next(
+        shard for shard in shards if shard["filename"].endswith("00001-of-00002.gguf")
+    )
+    geometry = dict(
+        layers=48,
+        experts_per_layer=512,
+        hidden=2560,
+        intermediate=640,
+        expert_bytes=33973862400,
+    )
+    if (
+        not isinstance(expert, Mapping)
+        or expert.get("format") != "q2_0"
+        or expert.get("scope") != "routed_experts_only"
+        or any(expert.get(k) != v for k, v in geometry.items())
+        or any(
+            expert.get(k) != first[k]
+            for k in ("repo", "revision", "sha256", "size", "verified_mtime_ns")
+        )
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "GGUF routed expert identity disagrees with its complete source"
+        )
+    expert_path = Path(expert.get("path", ""))
+    expert_path = expert_path if expert_path.is_absolute() else root / expert_path
+    first_path = Path(first["path"])
+    first_path = first_path if first_path.is_absolute() else root / first_path
+    if expert_path.resolve() != first_path.resolve():
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("GGUF expert path disagrees with shard one")
+    for name in ("tokenizer.json", "tokenizer_config.json", "generation_config.json"):
+        if not (root / name).is_file():
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(f"checkpoint is missing {name}")
+    if (root / "INVALID-EXPERT-ALIASES.json").exists() or (
+        root / "model.safetensors.index.json"
+    ).exists():
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "complete GGUF checkpoint must not contain an HF or alias weight index"
+        )
+    return FreeTokenCheckpoint(
+        root=root,
+        profile=profile.name,
+        profile_label=profile.label,
+        is_moe=True,
+        quant_format="q2_0",
+        shard_names=tuple(paths),
+        shard_bytes=total,
+        tokenizer_fingerprint=tuple(
+            entry for entry in fingerprint if entry[0] in _TOKENIZER_IDENTITY_NAMES
+        ),
+        generation_defaults=_generation_defaults(root, profile),
+        expert_geometry=geometry,
+        weight_layout="gguf_strata",
+    )
+
+
+def _validate_q2_expert_source(
+    root: Path, config: Mapping[str, Any]
+) -> Mapping[str, int]:
+    """校验已离线验真的完整专家与 HF 分片回执，异常统一为 checkpoint 错误。"""
+    source = config.get("freetoken_expert_source")
+    text = config.get("text_config", config)
+    geometry = {
+        "layers": text["num_hidden_layers"],
+        "experts_per_layer": text["num_experts"],
+        "hidden": text["hidden_size"],
+        "intermediate": text["moe_intermediate_size"],
+    }
+    geometry["expert_bytes"] = (
+        3
+        * geometry["layers"]
+        * geometry["experts_per_layer"]
+        * geometry["hidden"]
+        * geometry["intermediate"]
+        // 64
+        * 18
+    )
+    expected = {"format": "q2_0", "scope": "routed_experts_only", **geometry}
+    if not isinstance(source, Mapping) or any(
+        source.get(key) != value for key, value in expected.items()
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("Q2_0 expert source contract is incompatible")
+    if (
+        not isinstance(source.get("sha256"), str)
+        or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", source["sha256"]) is None
+        or not isinstance(source.get("size"), int)
+        or source["size"] < geometry["expert_bytes"]
+        or not source.get("repo")
+        or not source.get("revision")
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "Q2_0 expert source needs a complete checksum identity"
+        )
+    path_value = source.get("path")
+    if not isinstance(path_value, str) or not path_value:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("Q2_0 expert source path is missing")
+    path = Path(path_value)
+    if not path.is_absolute():
+        path = root / path
+    try:
+        info = path.stat()
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("Q2_0 expert source is unavailable") from exc
+    if (
+        not path.is_file()
+        or info.st_size != source["size"]
+        or info.st_mtime_ns != source.get("verified_mtime_ns")
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "Q2_0 expert source changed or has not completed checksum verification"
+        )
+    provenance = _read_object(root / "mixed-source.json", "mixed-source.json")
+    receipt = provenance.get("expert_receipt")
+    if (
+        not isinstance(receipt, Mapping)
+        or provenance.get("status")
+        != "complete_verified_sources_requires_inference_validation"
+        or provenance.get("expert_aliases") is not False
+        or provenance.get("expert_tensor_count") != 3 * geometry["layers"]
+        or provenance.get("expert_bytes") != geometry["expert_bytes"]
+        or any(
+            receipt.get(key) != source[key]
+            for key in ("sha256", "size", "repo", "revision")
+        )
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("Q2_0 mixed-source checkpoint is incomplete")
+    indexed = _read_object(
+        root / "model.safetensors.index.json", "model.safetensors.index.json"
+    ).get("weight_map")
+    if not isinstance(indexed, Mapping) or not indexed:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("checkpoint index has no non-empty weight_map")
+    names = tuple(indexed.values())
+    if any(
+        not isinstance(name, str)
+        or Path(name).name != name
+        or not name.endswith(".safetensors")
+        for name in names
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "checkpoint index contains a non-local safetensors shard name"
+        )
+    receipts = provenance.get("non_expert_files")
+    if not isinstance(receipts, Mapping) or set(names) != set(receipts):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "HF provenance does not cover all indexed non-expert shards"
+        )
+    for name, receipt in receipts.items():
+        if (
+            not isinstance(receipt, Mapping)
+            or not isinstance(receipt.get("sha256"), str)
+            or re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", receipt["sha256"]) is None
+        ):
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                f"HF non-expert checksum receipt is missing: {name}"
+            )
+        try:
+            info = (root / name).stat()
+        except OSError as exc:
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                f"HF non-expert source is unavailable: {name}"
+            ) from exc
+        if info.st_size != receipt.get("bytes") or info.st_mtime_ns != receipt.get(
+            "mtime_ns"
+        ):
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(f"HF non-expert source changed: {name}")
+    return geometry
+
+
+def _detect_quant_format(
+    quant: Mapping[str, Any],
+    *,
+    profile: _ProfileSpec,
+) -> QuantFormat:
+    """按 FreeToken 的稳定标记区分 block-FP8 与已认证 NVFP4 布局。"""
+
+    if (
+        quant.get("quant_method") == "fp8"
+        and quant.get("fmt") == "e4m3"
+        and quant.get("activation_scheme") == "dynamic"
+        and quant.get("weight_block_size") == [128, 128]
+    ):
+        return "fp8"
+
+    if profile.name == "qwen3.8-flash-next":
+        if _is_flash_next_nvfp4(quant):
+            return "nvfp4"
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "Flash Next quantization is not the supported RadixArk NVFP4 layout"
+        )
+
+    if (
+        quant.get("quant_method") == "modelopt"
+        and quant.get("quant_algo") == "MIXED_PRECISION"
+    ):
+        layers = quant.get("quantized_layers")
+        algorithms = (
+            {
+                value.get("quant_algo")
+                for value in layers.values()
+                if isinstance(value, Mapping)
+            }
+            if isinstance(layers, Mapping)
+            else set()
+        )
+        if profile.is_moe and {"W4A16_NVFP4", "FP8"}.issubset(algorithms):
+            return "nvfp4"
+        if profile.name == "qwen3.8-27b" and _is_qwen38_modelopt_nvfp4(quant):
+            return "nvfp4"
+
+    raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+        "checkpoint quantization is neither supported FP8 nor NVFP4"
+    )
+
+
+def _is_flash_next_nvfp4(quant: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
+    """只接受已实际验收的专家 NVFP4 布局，保持 attention、PLE 与 dense 排除项。"""
+    if quant.get("quant_method") != "modelopt" or quant.get("quant_algo") != "NVFP4":
+        return False
+    groups = quant.get("config_groups")
+    if not isinstance(groups, Mapping) or set(groups) != {"group_0"}:
+        return False
+    group = groups["group_0"]
+    if not isinstance(group, Mapping):
+        return False
+    scheme = {"dynamic": False, "group_size": 16, "num_bits": 4, "type": "float"}
+    return (
+        group.get("weights") == scheme
+        and group.get("input_activations") == scheme
+        and group.get("targets") == ["Linear"]
+        and quant.get("ignore")
+        == [
+            "model.embed_tokens",
+            "mtp.*",
+            "model.mtp.*",
+            "*.self_attn.*",
+            "*.linear_attn.*",
+            "*.mlp.gate*",
+            "*.mlp.shared_expert.*",
+            "*.mlp.shared_expert_gate*",
+            "*hyper_connection*",
+            "*.ple.*",
+            "model.visual.*",
+            "model.language_model.embed_tokens",
+            "lm_head",
+        ]
+    )
+
+
+def _is_qwen38_modelopt_nvfp4(quant: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
+    """匹配已验证的 RadixArk 64 层 ModelOpt 投影布局。"""
+
+    layers = quant.get("quantized_layers")
+    if not isinstance(layers, Mapping):
+        return False
+    expected: dict[str, tuple[str, int | None]] = {}
+    for index in range(64):
+        prefix = f"model.language_model.layers.{index}"
+        for projection in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
+            expected[f"{prefix}.mlp.{projection}"] = ("NVFP4", 16)
+        if index % 4 == 3:
+            for projection in ("q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"):
+                expected[f"{prefix}.self_attn.{projection}"] = ("FP8", None)
+        else:
+            for projection in ("in_proj_qkv", "in_proj_z", "out_proj"):
+                expected[f"{prefix}.linear_attn.{projection}"] = ("FP8", None)
+    expected["lm_head"] = ("NVFP4", 16)
+    if set(layers) != set(expected):
+        return False
+    for name, (algorithm, group_size) in expected.items():
+        item = layers.get(name)
+        if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
+            return False
+        actual_algorithm = item.get("quant_algo")
+        algorithm_matches = (
+            actual_algorithm in {"NVFP4", "W4A16_NVFP4"}
+            if algorithm == "NVFP4"
+            else actual_algorithm == algorithm
+        )
+        if not algorithm_matches or item.get("group_size") != group_size:
+            return False
+    return quant.get("kv_cache_scheme") == {
+        "dynamic": False,
+        "num_bits": 8,
+        "type": "float",
+    } and quant.get("ignore") == ["mtp*", "mtp.layers.0*"]
+
+
+def _profile_for_config(config: Mapping[str, Any]) -> _ProfileSpec:
+    """按 architecture 与 model_type 选择唯一受支持的模型合同。"""
+
+    architecture = config.get("architectures")
+    model_type = config.get("model_type")
+    for profile in _PROFILE_SPECS:
+        if architecture == [profile.architecture] and model_type == profile.model_type:
+            return profile
+    raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+        "checkpoint architecture/model_type is not a supported Qwen3.x profile"
+    )
+
+
+def _validate_model_shape(
+    config: Mapping[str, Any],
+    profile: _ProfileSpec,
+) -> None:
+    """拒绝复用同一 architecture 名但偏离已验证拓扑的模型。"""
+
+    text = config.get("text_config")
+    if not isinstance(text, Mapping):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError("checkpoint has no text_config object")
+    for name, expected in profile.text_values.items():
+        actual = text.get(name, 0) if name == "num_experts" else text.get(name)
+        if actual != expected:
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                f"checkpoint text_config.{name} does not match {profile.label}"
+            )
+    if tuple(text.get("layer_types") or ()) != profile.layer_types:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            f"checkpoint layer_types do not match {profile.label}"
+        )
+    if profile.name in {"qwen3.8-27b", "qwen3.8-flash-next"}:
+        rope = text.get("rope_parameters")
+        expected_rope = {
+            "partial_rotary_factor": 0.25,
+            "rope_theta": 10_000_000,
+            "rope_type": "default",
+            "mrope_interleaved": True,
+            "mrope_section": [11, 11, 10],
+        }
+        if not isinstance(rope, Mapping) or any(
+            rope.get(name) != expected for name, expected in expected_rope.items()
+        ):
+            raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+                f"checkpoint rope_parameters do not match {profile.label}"
+            )
+
+
+def _generation_defaults(
+    root: Path,
+    profile: _ProfileSpec,
+) -> Mapping[str, float | int | tuple[int, ...]]:
+    """冻结模型官方采样值，避免 worker 与 HTTP catalog 各自猜默认值。"""
+
+    raw = _read_object(root / "generation_config.json", "generation_config.json")
+    eos_value = raw.get("eos_token_id")
+    eos_values = (eos_value,) if isinstance(eos_value, int) else tuple(eos_value or ())
+    top_k = raw.get("top_k")
+    try:
+        temperature = float(raw["temperature"])
+        top_p = float(raw["top_p"])
+    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "checkpoint generation_config has invalid sampling defaults"
+        ) from exc
+    if (
+        raw.get("do_sample") is not True
+        or temperature != 1.0
+        or top_p != 0.95
+        or isinstance(top_k, bool)
+        or top_k != 20
+        or not eos_values
+        or any(
+            isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int)
+            for value in eos_values
+        )
+    ):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            f"checkpoint generation_config does not match {profile.label} defaults"
+        )
+    if profile.name == "qwen3.8-27b" and eos_values != (248_046, 248_044):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(
+            "checkpoint generation_config does not match Qwen3.8-27B defaults"
+        )
+    return {
+        "eos_token_ids": eos_values,
+        "temperature": temperature,
+        "top_p": top_p,
+        "top_k": top_k,
+    }
+
+
+def _read_object(path: Path, label: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
+    try:
+        value = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(f"cannot read checkpoint {label}") from exc
+    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        raise FreeTokenCheckpointError(f"checkpoint {label} must be a JSON object")
+    return value
+
+
+__all__ = [
+    "CUDA_CAPABILITY",
+    "DEFAULT_REQUEST_DEADLINE_SECONDS",
+    "FREETOKEN_VERSION",
+    "FREETOKEN_WHEEL_FILENAME",
+    "FreeTokenCheckpoint",
+    "FreeTokenCheckpointError",
+    "MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS",
+    "MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS",
+    "ModelProfile",
+    "PYTHON_MINOR",
+    "QuantFormat",
+    "SUPPORTED_REQUEST_FIELDS",
+    "TORCH_CUDA_VERSION",
+    "TORCH_VERSION",
+    "UNSUPPORTED_REQUEST_FIELDS",
+    "inspect_freetoken_checkpoint",
+]

+"""当前 FreeToken 引擎的流式、取消与构造桥接；导入时不加载推理依赖。"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import queue
+import sys
+import threading
+from typing import Any
+
+from .options import LLM_OPTION_NAMES
+
+
+class _EngineReplyFailure(RuntimeError):
+    """FreeToken scheduler 返回未知或 engine 级错误。"""
+
+
+class _WorkerShutdown(Exception):
+    """让长期 scheduler loop 在 command pipe 关闭或 shutdown 时退出。"""
+
+
+def _startup_log(message: str) -> None:
+    """把不含本地路径的启动阶段写入受限 stderr tail，便于超时定位。"""
+
+    print(f"[silitok-freetoken-worker] {message}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
+
+
+def _build_streaming_llm_class(writer):
+    """在 cache 环境锁定后导入 FreeToken，并扩展流式/取消离线 API。"""
+
+    from .worker import _RequestControl, _WorkerState
+    from freetoken.llm import LLM
+    from freetoken.message import (
+        AbortBackendMsg,
+        DetokenizeMsg,
+        ErrorReplyMsg,
+        PromptAdmittedMsg,
+    )
+    from freetoken.server.reasoning_parser import ReasoningParser
+    from freetoken.tokenizer.detokenize import DetokenizeManager
+
+    class StreamingLLM(LLM):
+        """把 offline scheduler 的 token 与协作式取消投影到 Speed wire。"""
+
+        def __init__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
+            self._worker_state: _WorkerState | None = None
+            self._sampling_params_type: Any = None
+            self._control: _RequestControl | None = None
+            self._active_uid: int | None = None
+            self._abort_sent = False
+            self._detokenizer: Any = None
+            self._reasoning_parser: Any = None
+            self._reasoning_enabled = True
+            self._reasoning_parts: list[str] = []
+            self._content_parts: list[str] = []
+            self._prompt_tokens: int | None = None
+            self._output_tokens = 0
+            self._finish_reason: str | None = None
+            super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
+
+        def begin_request(
+            self,
+            control: _RequestControl,
+            *,
+            enable_thinking: bool,
+        ) -> None:
+            """为一个 Speed 请求重建不跨请求共享的解析与计数状态。"""
+
+            self._control = control
+            self._abort_sent = False
+            self._detokenizer = DetokenizeManager(
+                self.tokenizer,
+                frozenset(self.eos_token_ids),
+            )
+            self._reasoning_parser = ReasoningParser(
+                "qwen3",
+                force_reasoning=enable_thinking,
+            )
+            self._reasoning_enabled = enable_thinking
+            self._reasoning_parts = []
+            self._content_parts = []
+            self._prompt_tokens = None
+            self._output_tokens = 0
+            self._finish_reason = None
+
+        def serve_forever(self, state: _WorkerState, SamplingParams) -> None:
+            """只进入一次 native loop，以命令队列承载连续 Speed 请求。"""
+
+            self._worker_state = state
+            self._sampling_params_type = SamplingParams
+            try:
+                self.run_forever()
+            except _WorkerShutdown:
+                return
+            finally:
+                self._worker_state = None
+                self._sampling_params_type = None
+
+        def offline_receive_msg(self, blocking: bool = False):
+            """在 scheduler 每步轮询点注入取消，idle 时读取下一条命令。"""
+
+            state = self._worker_state
+            if state is None:
+                raise _EngineReplyFailure("FreeToken worker runtime is not bound")
+            control = self._control
+            if (
+                control is not None
+                and control.cancelled.is_set()
+                and not self._abort_sent
+                and self._active_uid is not None
+            ):
+                self._abort_sent = True
+                return [AbortBackendMsg(uid=self._active_uid)]
+            if control is not None:
+                return []
+
+            command = self._next_command(state, blocking=blocking)
+            if command is None:
+                return []
+            command_type = command.get("type")
+            if command_type == "shutdown":
+                raise _WorkerShutdown()
+            if command_type != "generate":
+                raise ValueError(
+                    f"unsupported FreeToken worker command: {command_type!r}"
+                )
+            self._begin_command(state, command)
+            messages = super().offline_receive_msg(False)
+            if len(messages) != 1:
+                raise _EngineReplyFailure(
+                    "FreeToken did not admit exactly one offline request"
+                )
+            uid = getattr(messages[0], "uid", None)
+            if isinstance(uid, bool) or not isinstance(uid, int):
+                raise _EngineReplyFailure("FreeToken admitted an invalid request id")
+            self._active_uid = uid
+            return messages
+
+        def _next_command(
+            self,
+            state: _WorkerState,
+            *,
+            blocking: bool,
+        ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
+            try:
+                command = state.commands.get(block=blocking)
+            except queue.Empty:
+                return None
+            if command is None:
+                if state.reader_error is not None:
+                    raise state.reader_error
+                raise _WorkerShutdown()
+            return command
+
+        def _begin_command(
+            self,
+            state: _WorkerState,
+            command: dict[str, Any],
+        ) -> None:
+            request_id = command.get("request_id")
+            if not isinstance(request_id, str) or not request_id:
+                raise ValueError("worker request_id must be a non-empty string")
+            enable_thinking = command.get("enable_thinking")
+            if not isinstance(enable_thinking, bool):
+                raise ValueError("worker enable_thinking must be a boolean")
+            prompt = _prompt_tokens(self.tokenizer, command)
+            params = _sampling_params(self._sampling_params_type, command)
+            control = _RequestControl(request_id, threading.Event())
+            state.set_active(control)
+            self.begin_request(
+                control,
+                enable_thinking=enable_thinking,
+            )
+            self.pending_requests = [(prompt, params, None)]
+
+        def offline_send_result(self, reply) -> None:
+            """分类 scheduler 回包；未知错误使整个 one-shot worker 失效。"""
+
+            for message in reply:
+                uid = getattr(message, "uid", None)
+                if uid != self._active_uid:
+                    raise _EngineReplyFailure(
+                        "FreeToken returned a reply for the wrong request"
+                    )
+                if isinstance(message, PromptAdmittedMsg):
+                    self._prompt_tokens = int(message.prompt_tokens)
+                    continue
+                if isinstance(message, DetokenizeMsg):
+                    self._emit_token(message)
+                    if message.finished:
+                        writer.send(self.finish_request())
+                        self._finish_active()
+                    continue
+                if isinstance(message, ErrorReplyMsg):
+                    if message.error == "request aborted" and message.code is None:
+                        if self._detokenizer is not None:
+                            self._detokenizer.discard(message.uid)
+                        writer.send(
+                            {
+                                "type": "cancelled",
+                                "request_id": self._control.request_id,
+                            }
+                        )
+                        self._finish_active()
+                        continue
+                    if message.code == "context_length_exceeded":
+                        if self._detokenizer is not None:
+                            self._detokenizer.discard(message.uid)
+                        writer.send(
+                            {
+                                "type": "request_error",
+                                "request_id": self._control.request_id,
+                                "code": "context_length_exceeded",
+                                "message": (
+                                    "Qwen3.x prompt exceeds the configured "
+                                    "context window"
+                                ),
+                            }
+                        )
+                        self._finish_active()
+                        continue
+                    raise _EngineReplyFailure(
+                        "FreeToken returned an unclassified scheduler error"
+                    )
+                raise _EngineReplyFailure(
+                    f"FreeToken returned unexpected {type(message).__name__}"
+                )
+
+        def _finish_active(self) -> None:
+            state = self._worker_state
+            control = self._control
+            uid = self._active_uid
+            if state is None or control is None or uid is None:
+                raise _EngineReplyFailure(
+                    "FreeToken tried to finish an inactive request"
+                )
+            state.finish(control)
+            self.status_map.pop(uid, None)
+            self.pending_requests = []
+            self._control = None
+            self._active_uid = None
+            self._detokenizer = None
+            self._reasoning_parser = None
+
+        def _emit_token(self, message) -> None:
+            if message.finished and message.finish_reason not in {"stop", "length"}:
+                raise _EngineReplyFailure(
+                    "FreeToken returned an invalid terminal finish reason"
+                )
+            assert self._detokenizer is not None
+            assert self._reasoning_parser is not None
+            delta = self._detokenizer.detokenize([message])[0]
+            reasoning, content = self._reasoning_parser.parse_stream_chunk(delta)
+            if reasoning:
+                self._reasoning_parts.append(reasoning)
+            if content:
+                self._content_parts.append(content)
+            if message.finished:
+                self._finish_reason = message.finish_reason
+            terminal_eos = message.finished and message.next_token in self.eos_token_ids
+            if terminal_eos:
+                if reasoning or content:
+                    writer.send(
+                        {
+                            "type": "delta",
+                            "request_id": self._control.request_id,
+                            "delta_reasoning": reasoning,
+                            "delta_text": content,
+                        }
+                    )
+                return
+            self._output_tokens += 1
+            writer.send(
+                {
+                    "type": "token",
+                    "request_id": self._control.request_id,
+                    "token_id": int(message.next_token),
+                    "delta_reasoning": reasoning,
+                    "delta_text": content,
+                    "is_final_token": bool(message.finished),
+                }
+            )
+
+        def finish_request(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+            """冲洗推理解析器，并生成父进程使用的稳定 final 投影。"""
+
+            assert self._control is not None
+            assert self._reasoning_parser is not None
+            reasoning, content = self._reasoning_parser.flush()
+            if reasoning:
+                self._reasoning_parts.append(reasoning)
+            if content:
+                self._content_parts.append(content)
+            if reasoning or content:
+                writer.send(
+                    {
+                        "type": "delta",
+                        "request_id": self._control.request_id,
+                        "delta_reasoning": reasoning,
+                        "delta_text": content,
+                    }
+                )
+            if self._prompt_tokens is None or self._finish_reason is None:
+                _startup_log(
+                    "terminal accounting incomplete "
+                    f"prompt={self._prompt_tokens is not None} "
+                    f"finish={self._finish_reason is not None} "
+                    f"tokens={self._output_tokens}"
+                )
+                raise _EngineReplyFailure(
+                    "FreeToken generation ended without terminal accounting"
+                )
+            return {
+                "type": "final",
+                "request_id": self._control.request_id,
+                "reasoning_content": (
+                    "".join(self._reasoning_parts) if self._reasoning_enabled else None
+                ),
+                "text": "".join(self._content_parts),
+                "finish_reason": self._finish_reason,
+                "usage": {
+                    "input_tokens": self._prompt_tokens,
+                    "output_tokens": self._output_tokens,
+                },
+            }
+
+    return StreamingLLM
+
+
+def _worker_options(command: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """私有管道接收同版本 backend 已校验的参数；跨进程重新核对可变权重。"""
+    options = command["options"]
+    if options["mtp_runtime_path"] is not None:
+        from silitok_speed.runtimes.freetoken.mtp import inspect_mtp_runtime
+
+        _, digest = inspect_mtp_runtime(options["mtp_runtime_path"])
+        if digest != options["mtp_runtime_manifest_sha256"]:
+            raise ValueError("worker MTP runtime differs from the load command")
+    elif options["mtp_runtime_manifest_sha256"] is not None:
+        raise ValueError("worker disabled MTP must not carry a weight identity")
+    return options
+
+
+def _llm_kwargs(options: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """将 Speed IPC 参数投影到 0.1.3 配置，固定文本接口的视觉关闭语义。"""
+    import torch
+    from freetoken.mm.config import ENCODER_KINDS, MultimodalConfig
+
+    return dict(
+        **{target: options[name] for name, target in LLM_OPTION_NAMES.items()},
+        dtype=torch.bfloat16,
+        max_running_req=1,
+        # 0.1.3 默认启用视觉编码器；Speed 当前协议只接受文本消息。
+        mm=MultimodalConfig(disabled_encoders=frozenset(ENCODER_KINDS)),
+        quant_backend=(
+            None
+            if options["nvfp4_backend"] == "auto"
+            else f"moe.nvfp4={options['nvfp4_backend']}"
+        ),
+        mtp_max_context=8192,
+    )
+
+
+def _build_llm(command: dict[str, Any], writer):
+    """验证同环境 AOT 制品后，在当前主线程创建唯一 FreeToken Engine。"""
+
+    from silitok_speed.runtimes.freetoken.runtime import (
+        activate_runtime,
+        verify_kernel_cache,
+        verify_runtime_identity,
+    )
+
+    model_path = command.get("model_path")
+    cache_path = command.get("kernel_cache")
+    if not isinstance(model_path, str) or not isinstance(cache_path, str):
+        raise ValueError("worker load command has invalid model/cache paths")
+    options = _worker_options(command)
+    activate_runtime()
+    identity = verify_kernel_cache(cache_path)
+    _startup_log("kernel cache verified")
+    os.environ["FREETOKEN_KERNEL_CACHE_DIR"] = str(identity.root)
+    os.environ["FREETOKEN_DISABLE_JIT"] = "1"
+    os.environ.pop("FREETOKEN_DISABLE_VERSION_CHECK", None)
+
+    _startup_log("importing torch")
+    import torch
+
+    _startup_log("torch imported; importing FreeToken core")
+    from silitok_speed.runtimes.freetoken.runtime import verify_runtime_abi
+
+    verify_runtime_abi(torch_version=torch.__version__, torch_cuda=torch.version.cuda)
+    from freetoken.core import SamplingParams
+
+    _startup_log("FreeToken core imported; importing streaming runtime")
+
+    if torch.cuda.is_initialized():
+        raise RuntimeError("FreeToken must be the worker process's first CUDA owner")
+    StreamingLLM = _build_streaming_llm_class(writer)
+    _startup_log("runtime imports verified; constructing engine")
+    if os.name == "nt" or options["attention_backend"] == "triton":
+        import freetoken.kernel.backend as freetoken_backend
+
+        freetoken_backend.is_flashinfer_installed = lambda: False
+
+    llm = StreamingLLM(model_path, **_llm_kwargs(options))
+    _startup_log("engine constructed; verifying CUDA identity")
+    verify_runtime_identity(
+        torch_version=torch.__version__,
+        torch_cuda=torch.version.cuda,
+        cuda_capability=tuple(torch.cuda.get_device_capability(0)),
+    )
+    _startup_log("CUDA identity verified")
+    return llm, SamplingParams, torch, identity
+
+
+def _prompt_tokens(tokenizer, command: dict[str, Any]) -> list[int]:
+    """使用 checkpoint 自带模板生成与 FreeToken offline API 对齐的 token ids。"""
+
+    messages = command.get("messages")
+    enable_thinking = command.get("enable_thinking")
+    if not isinstance(messages, list) or not isinstance(enable_thinking, bool):
+        raise ValueError("worker generate command has invalid chat inputs")
+    encoded = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
+        messages,
+        add_generation_prompt=True,
+        tokenize=True,
+        enable_thinking=enable_thinking,
+        return_dict=False,
+    )
+    if hasattr(encoded, "tolist"):
+        encoded = encoded.tolist()
+    if encoded and isinstance(encoded[0], list):
+        if len(encoded) != 1:
+            raise ValueError("Qwen3.x tokenizer returned a batch")
+        encoded = encoded[0]
+    if not isinstance(encoded, list) or any(
+        isinstance(token, bool) or not isinstance(token, int) for token in encoded
+    ):
+        raise ValueError("Qwen3.x tokenizer returned invalid token ids")
+    return encoded
+
+
+def _sampling_params(SamplingParams, command: dict[str, Any]):
+    sampling = command.get("sampling")
+    if not isinstance(sampling, dict):
+        raise ValueError("worker generate command has no sampling object")
+    required = {"temperature", "top_p", "top_k", "max_tokens", "stop"}
+    if set(sampling) != required:
+        raise ValueError("worker sampling fields do not match the protocol contract")
+    temperature = sampling["temperature"]
+    top_p = sampling["top_p"]
+    top_k = sampling["top_k"]
+    max_tokens = sampling["max_tokens"]
+    stop = sampling["stop"]
+    if (
+        isinstance(temperature, bool)
+        or not isinstance(temperature, (int, float))
+        or not 0 <= temperature <= 2
+        or isinstance(top_p, bool)
+        or not isinstance(top_p, (int, float))
+        or not 0 <= top_p <= 1
+        or isinstance(top_k, bool)
+        or not isinstance(top_k, int)
+        or top_k < 0
+        or isinstance(max_tokens, bool)
+        or not isinstance(max_tokens, int)
+        or max_tokens < 1
+        or not isinstance(stop, list)
+        or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in stop)
+    ):
+        raise ValueError("worker sampling values are invalid")
+    temperature = float(temperature)
+    top_p = float(top_p)
+    if temperature <= 0:
+        top_p = 1.0
+        top_k = 1
+    return SamplingParams(
+        temperature=temperature,
+        top_p=top_p,
+        top_k=top_k,
+        max_tokens=max_tokens,
+        stop_strs=stop,
+    )

+"""可选 MTP 预测层的来源、资源及启动回执合同；预检不导入 GPU 框架。"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from functools import lru_cache
+import hashlib
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from .checkpoint import RELEASE
+
+
+@lru_cache(maxsize=4)
+def _verify_payloads(root: Path, signatures: tuple) -> None:
+    """只缓存同一物理文件版本的完整 SHA，文件变化后重新核验预测层。"""
+    for name, size, digest, mtime, inode in signatures:
+        path = root / name
+        with path.open("rb") as stream:
+            actual = hashlib.file_digest(stream, "sha256").hexdigest()
+        after = path.stat()
+        if actual != digest or (after.st_size, after.st_mtime_ns, after.st_ino) != (
+            size,
+            mtime,
+            inode,
+        ):
+            raise ValueError("MTP weight identity changed: " + name)
+
+
+def inspect_mtp_runtime(value: str) -> tuple[Path, str]:
+    """将固定 BF16 来源的 FreeToken 派生目录核验到 31 份真实文件。"""
+    root = Path(value).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
+    data = (root / "runtime.json").read_bytes()
+    expected = RELEASE["mtp_runtime"]
+    digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
+    if digest != expected["manifest_sha256"]:
+        raise ValueError("MTP runtime manifest differs from the frozen release")
+    manifest = json.loads(data)
+    source = manifest["source"]
+    rows = manifest["tensors"]
+    if (
+        source["repository"] != expected["source_repository"]
+        or source["revision"] != expected["source_revision"]
+        or len(rows) != expected["tensor_count"]
+        or sum(r["bytes"] for r in rows) != expected["tensor_bytes"]
+    ):
+        raise ValueError("MTP runtime source or tensor geometry differs")
+    signatures = []
+    for row in rows:
+        path = root / row["path"]
+        stat = path.stat()
+        if not path.is_file() or stat.st_size != row["bytes"]:
+            raise ValueError(
+                "MTP runtime tensor is missing or incomplete: " + row["name"]
+            )
+        signatures.append(
+            (row["path"], row["bytes"], row["sha256"], stat.st_mtime_ns, stat.st_ino)
+        )
+    _verify_payloads(root, tuple(signatures))
+    return root, digest
+
+
+def expected_mtp_ready(
+    manifest_sha256: str | None,
+    *,
+    compact_state: bool = False,
+    state_cache_slots: int | None = None,
+    max_prefill_length: int = 4096,
+    draft_policy: str = "full",
+) -> dict:
+    """按显式启动几何核算物理张量，保持预测层身份与事务所有权校验。"""
+    if manifest_sha256 is None:
+        return {"enabled": False}
+    spec = RELEASE["mtp_runtime"]
+    keys = (
+        "draft_context_tokens",
+        "max_draft_tokens",
+        "draft_owned_bytes",
+        "transaction_slots",
+        "linear_physical_slots",
+        "linear_state_bytes",
+        "auxiliary_kv_bytes",
+        "residual_capture_bytes",
+    )
+    expected = {
+        "enabled": True,
+        "runtime_manifest_sha256": manifest_sha256,
+        "greedy_only": True,
+        "target_graph_windows": [1, 2, 3, 4],
+        **{k: spec[k] for k in keys},
+    }
+    if draft_policy == "paired_cjk":
+        # 索引表是草稿器独占的int64张量，必须计入真实所有权，不能放宽总量核验。
+        expected["draft_owned_bytes"] += spec["paired_draft_vocab"]["rows"] * 8
+    layout = spec["linear_state_layout"]
+    logical = (
+        spec["linear_physical_slots"]
+        + (4 if state_cache_slots is None else state_cache_slots)
+        - 4
+    )
+    recurrent = logical - (spec["transaction_slots"] if compact_state else 0)
+    expected.update(
+        compact_state=compact_state,
+        linear_physical_slots=logical,
+        linear_recurrent_slots=recurrent,
+        linear_state_bytes=(
+            logical * layout["other_slot_bytes"]
+            + recurrent * layout["recurrent_slot_bytes"]
+            + (layout["compact_update_bytes"] if compact_state else 0)
+        ),
+        residual_capture_bytes=spec["residual_capture_bytes"]
+        * max_prefill_length
+        // 4096,
+    )
+    return expected
+
+
+def actual_mtp_ready(engine) -> dict:
+    """从引擎实际持有的张量、专属槽和 graph 核验 MTP 启动状态。"""
+    runtime = getattr(engine, "mtp_runtime", None)
+    if runtime is None:
+        return {"enabled": False}
+    _, digest = inspect_mtp_runtime(engine.config.mtp_runtime_path)
+    pool = engine.linear_state_pool
+    state_bytes = (
+        pool.conv_states.nbytes
+        + pool.recurrent_states.nbytes
+        + sum(t.nbytes for t in pool.slot_states.values())
+    )
+    if pool.compact_mtp:
+        state_bytes += sum(
+            getattr(pool, name).nbytes
+            for name in (
+                "_compact_keys",
+                "_compact_delta",
+                "_compact_decay",
+                "_compact_source",
+            )
+        )
+    return dict(
+        enabled=True,
+        runtime_manifest_sha256=digest,
+        greedy_only=True,
+        draft_context_tokens=runtime.draft.max_context,
+        max_draft_tokens=max(runtime.draft_graphs.chains),
+        draft_owned_bytes=runtime.draft.owned_bytes,
+        transaction_slots=len(pool.reserved_slot_ids),
+        linear_physical_slots=pool.num_slots,
+        linear_state_bytes=state_bytes,
+        compact_state=pool.compact_mtp,
+        linear_recurrent_slots=pool.recurrent_states.shape[1],
+        auxiliary_kv_bytes=runtime.aux.owned_bytes,
+        residual_capture_bytes=runtime.raw.nbytes,
+        target_graph_windows=sorted(runtime.graphs),
+    )

+"""父进程和 worker 共用的引擎字段映射；不导入 Torch 或 FreeToken。"""
+
+# 只在此处维护同名字段，避免新调优项在 IPC 或 LLM 构造时漏传。

+)

+def worker_options_payload(options) -> dict:
+    """序列化已在 backend 入口校验的配置，单独携带预测层字节身份。"""
+    payload = {name: getattr(options, name) for name in LLM_OPTION_NAMES}
+    payload["mtp_runtime_path"] = (
+        str(options.mtp_runtime_path) if options.mtp_runtime_path is not None else None
+    )
+    payload["mtp_runtime_manifest_sha256"] = options.mtp_manifest_sha256
+    payload["nvfp4_backend"] = options.nvfp4_backend
+    return payload

+"""Speed 与 FreeToken worker 之间的版本化匿名管道协议。"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import struct
+import threading
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from typing import Any, BinaryIO
+
+
+PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1
+MAX_FRAME_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024
+_HEADER = struct.Struct(">I")
+
+
+class WireProtocolError(RuntimeError):
+    """worker 管道出现截断、越界或非对象消息。"""
+
+
+class FrameWriter:
+    """串行写入长度前缀 JSON，允许控制线程与 runtime 线程共同回报。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, stream: BinaryIO) -> None:
+        self._stream = stream
+        self._lock = threading.Lock()
+
+    def send(self, message: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
+        """原子写入一帧；版本字段由协议层统一补齐。"""
+
+        document = dict(message)
+        document["v"] = PROTOCOL_VERSION
+        try:
+            payload = json.dumps(
+                document,
+                ensure_ascii=False,
+                allow_nan=False,
+                separators=(",", ":"),
+            ).encode("utf-8")
+        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
+            raise WireProtocolError(
+                "FreeToken IPC frame contains a non-JSON value"
+            ) from exc
+        if len(payload) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
+            raise WireProtocolError(
+                f"FreeToken IPC frame exceeds {MAX_FRAME_BYTES} bytes"
+            )
+        with self._lock:
+            _write_all(self._stream, _HEADER.pack(len(payload)))
+            _write_all(self._stream, payload)
+            self._stream.flush()
+
+
+def read_frame(stream: BinaryIO) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
+    """读取一帧；干净 EOF 返回 None，半帧 EOF 与非法 JSON 立即失败。"""
+
+    header = _read_exact(stream, _HEADER.size, allow_clean_eof=True)
+    if header is None:
+        return None
+    (size,) = _HEADER.unpack(header)
+    if size <= 0 or size > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
+        raise WireProtocolError(f"FreeToken IPC frame length is invalid: {size}")
+    payload = _read_exact(stream, size, allow_clean_eof=False)
+    assert payload is not None
+    try:
+        document = json.loads(
+            payload.decode("utf-8"),
+            parse_constant=_reject_json_constant,
+        )
+    except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
+        raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken IPC frame is not valid UTF-8 JSON") from exc
+    if not isinstance(document, dict):
+        raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken IPC frame must be a JSON object")
+    if document.get("v") != PROTOCOL_VERSION:
+        raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken IPC protocol version does not match Speed")
+    return document
+
+
+def _reject_json_constant(value: str) -> None:
+    raise ValueError(f"non-finite JSON number {value!r}")
+
+
+def _write_all(stream: BinaryIO, payload: bytes) -> None:
+    """raw pipe 允许短写；完整推进一帧，零进展立即判为协议失败。"""
+
+    remaining = memoryview(payload)
+    while remaining:
+        written = stream.write(remaining)
+        if isinstance(written, bool) or not isinstance(written, int) or written <= 0:
+            raise WireProtocolError("FreeToken IPC stream made no write progress")
+        remaining = remaining[written:]
+
+
+def _read_exact(
+    stream: BinaryIO,
+    size: int,
+    *,
+    allow_clean_eof: bool,
+) -> bytes | None:
+    chunks: list[bytes] = []
+    remaining = size
+    while remaining:
+        chunk = stream.read(remaining)
+        if not chunk:
+            if allow_clean_eof and remaining == size:
+                return None
+            raise WireProtocolError(
+                "FreeToken IPC stream ended in the middle of a frame"
+            )
+        chunks.append(chunk)
+        remaining -= len(chunk)
+    return b"".join(chunks)
+
+
+__all__ = [
+    "FrameWriter",
+    "MAX_FRAME_BYTES",
+    "PROTOCOL_VERSION",
+    "WireProtocolError",
+    "read_frame",
+]

new file mode 100644
index 00000000..5bfaf181

+++ b/silitok_speed/runtimes/freetoken/runtime.py
@@ -0,0 +1,179 @@
+"""External FreeToken runtime and CUDA 13 AOT cache requirements."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import sys
+import hashlib
+import json
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, distributions, version
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from silitok_speed.config import get_config
+from .checkpoint import (
+    RELEASE,
+    CUDA_CAPABILITY,
+    FREETOKEN_VERSION,
+    PYTHON_MINOR,
+    TORCH_CUDA_VERSION,
+    TORCH_VERSION,
+)
+
+KERNEL_CACHE_DIR = (
+    Path(__file__).parents[2]
+    / "qwen36"
+    / RELEASE["kernel_cache"].get("package_path", "kernel_cache")
+)
+_MODEL_FAMILIES = (
+    "Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B",
+    "Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-FP8",
+    "nvidia/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-NVFP4",
+    "Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8",
+    "RadixArk/Qwen3.8-27B-NVFP4",
+    "RadixArk/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-NVFP4",
+)
+
+_EXPECTED_SPECS = tuple(RELEASE["spec_names"])
+
+
+class KernelCacheError(RuntimeError):
+    """An external runtime dependency is missing or incompatible."""
+
+
+def runtime_directory() -> Path | None:
+    """Resolve the configured FreeToken import root; None uses site-packages."""
+    configured = get_config().freetoken_runtime_dir
+    if configured is None:
+        return None
+    root = Path(configured).expanduser().resolve()
+    if not (root / "freetoken" / "__init__.py").is_file():
+        raise KernelCacheError(f"FreeToken runtime is unavailable: {root}")
+    return root
+
+
+def activate_runtime() -> None:
+    """Only the isolated worker/self-check changes its import search path."""
+    root = runtime_directory()
+    if root is not None:
+        sys.path.insert(0, str(root))
+
+
+def runtime_version() -> str:
+    """Check external metadata without importing FreeToken into the server."""
+    root = runtime_directory()
+    if root is not None:
+        for dist in distributions(path=[str(root)]):
+            if dist.metadata["Name"] == "freetoken":
+                return dist.version
+        raise KernelCacheError(f"FreeToken metadata is unavailable: {root}")
+    try:
+        return version("freetoken")
+    except PackageNotFoundError as exc:
+        raise KernelCacheError("FreeToken is not installed or configured") from exc
+
+
+def kernel_cache_directory(root: str | Path | None = None) -> Path:
+    if root is None:
+        root = get_config().freetoken_kernel_cache_dir
+    if root is None:
+        if KERNEL_CACHE_DIR.is_dir():
+            return KERNEL_CACHE_DIR.resolve()
+        raise KernelCacheError(
+            "Set service.freetoken_kernel_cache_dir before using FreeToken"
+        )
+    return Path(root).expanduser().resolve()
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class KernelCacheIdentity:
+    root: Path
+    artifact_count: int
+
+
+def verify_kernel_cache(root: str | Path | None = None) -> KernelCacheIdentity:
+    """Check required DLLs and package version; no hash manifest is needed."""
+    cache = kernel_cache_directory(root)
+    if runtime_version() != FREETOKEN_VERSION:
+        raise KernelCacheError(
+            f"FreeToken runtime requires version {FREETOKEN_VERSION}"
+        )
+    for spec in _EXPECTED_SPECS:
+        artifact = cache / spec / f"{spec}.dll"
+        if not artifact.is_file() or artifact.stat().st_size == 0:
+            raise KernelCacheError(
+                f"FreeToken kernel artifact is unavailable: {artifact}"
+            )
+    return KernelCacheIdentity(root=cache, artifact_count=len(_EXPECTED_SPECS))
+
+
+def generate_kernel_manifest(root: str | Path) -> Path:
+    """按已批准 DLL 哈希验证构建输入，再生成同一 ABI 的发布库存。"""
+    cache = Path(root).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
+    artifacts = []
+    for spec in _EXPECTED_SPECS:
+        relative = f"{spec}/{spec}.dll"
+        artifact = cache / relative
+        approved = RELEASE["kernel_artifacts"][relative]
+        if not artifact.is_file() or artifact.stat().st_size != approved["size"]:
+            raise KernelCacheError(f"FreeToken kernel artifact changed: {artifact}")
+        with artifact.open("rb") as file:
+            if hashlib.file_digest(file, "sha256").hexdigest() != approved["sha256"]:
+                raise KernelCacheError(f"FreeToken kernel artifact changed: {artifact}")
+        artifacts.append({"path": relative, "size": approved["size"]})
+    manifest = cache / "speed-kernel-manifest.json"
+    manifest.write_text(
+        json.dumps(
+            {
+                "schema_version": 1,
+                "freetoken_version": FREETOKEN_VERSION,
+                "torch_version": TORCH_VERSION,
+                "torch_cuda": TORCH_CUDA_VERSION,
+                "python_minor": list(PYTHON_MINOR),
+                "cuda_capabilities": [list(CUDA_CAPABILITY)],
+                "spec_names": list(_EXPECTED_SPECS),
+                "artifacts": artifacts,
+            },
+            indent=2,
+        )
+        + "\n",
+        encoding="utf-8",
+    )
+    return manifest
+
+
+def verify_runtime_identity(
+    *,
+    torch_version: str,
+    torch_cuda: str | None,
+    cuda_capability: tuple[int, int],
+) -> None:
+    """Keep the ABI checks before loading native code."""
+    verify_runtime_abi(torch_version=torch_version, torch_cuda=torch_cuda)
+    if cuda_capability != CUDA_CAPABILITY:
+        raise KernelCacheError("FreeToken Qwen3.x kernels require an SM120 GPU")
+
+
+__all__ = [
+    "KERNEL_CACHE_DIR",
+    "KernelCacheError",
+    "KernelCacheIdentity",
+    "generate_kernel_manifest",
+    "kernel_cache_directory",
+    "runtime_directory",
+    "activate_runtime",
+    "verify_kernel_cache",
+    "verify_runtime_identity",
+]
+
+
+def verify_runtime_abi(*, torch_version: str, torch_cuda: str | None) -> None:
+    """加载 FreeToken 原生模块前检查解释器、Torch、Triton 和 TVM ABI。"""
+    if sys.version_info[:2] != PYTHON_MINOR:
+        raise KernelCacheError("FreeToken worker requires CPython 3.12")
+    if torch_version != TORCH_VERSION or torch_cuda != TORCH_CUDA_VERSION:
+        raise KernelCacheError("FreeToken worker Torch/CUDA runtime is incompatible")
+    if (
+        version("triton-windows") != RELEASE["triton_windows"]
+        or version("apache-tvm-ffi") != RELEASE["apache_tvm_ffi"]
+    ):
+        raise KernelCacheError("FreeToken worker Triton/TVM runtime is incompatible")

+"""受管 worker 的匿名管道与后台读取线程，不触碰模型或 CUDA。"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import queue
+import subprocess
+import threading
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from typing import Any
+
+from .protocol import FrameWriter, WireProtocolError, read_frame
+
+_STDERR_TAIL_BYTES = 32 * 1024
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class _TransportClosed:
+    error: BaseException | None
+
+
+class _WorkerTransport:
+    """匿名管道传输；后台线程只搬运字节，不触碰 backend 状态。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, process: subprocess.Popen[bytes]) -> None:
+        if process.stdin is None or process.stdout is None or process.stderr is None:
+            raise ValueError("FreeToken worker pipes are unavailable")
+        self.process = process
+        self.writer = FrameWriter(process.stdin)
+        self._stdin = process.stdin
+        self._stdout = process.stdout
+        self._stderr = process.stderr
+        self._messages: queue.Queue[dict[str, Any] | _TransportClosed] = queue.Queue()
+        self._stderr_tail = bytearray()
+        self._stderr_lock = threading.Lock()
+        self._reader = threading.Thread(
+            target=self._read_messages,
+            name=f"freetoken-ipc-reader-{process.pid}",
+            daemon=True,
+        )
+        self._log_reader = threading.Thread(
+            target=self._read_stderr,
+            name=f"freetoken-stderr-reader-{process.pid}",
+            daemon=True,
+        )
+        self._reader.start()
+        self._log_reader.start()
+
+    def send(self, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
+        """向仍存活的精确 worker 写入一个版本化命令。"""
+
+        if self.process.poll() is not None:
+            raise ConnectionError("FreeToken worker has already exited")
+        try:
+            self.writer.send(message)
+        except (BrokenPipeError, OSError, WireProtocolError) as exc:
+            raise ConnectionError("FreeToken worker command pipe failed") from exc
+
+    def receive(self, timeout_s: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """有界等待一条 worker 消息；EOF 和协议破损作为传输失败传播。"""
+
+        try:
+            item = self._messages.get(timeout=max(0.0, timeout_s))
+        except queue.Empty as exc:
+            raise TimeoutError("FreeToken worker response timed out") from exc
+        if isinstance(item, _TransportClosed):
+            error = item.error
+            if error is None:
+                raise ConnectionError("FreeToken worker response pipe closed")
+            raise ConnectionError("FreeToken worker response pipe failed") from error
+        return item
+
+    def stderr_tail(self) -> str:
+        with self._stderr_lock:
+            raw = bytes(self._stderr_tail)
+        return raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
+
+    def close_streams(self) -> None:
+        """进程终止后关闭父进程持有的三条管道端点。"""
+
+        for stream in (self._stdin, self._stdout, self._stderr):
+            try:
+                stream.close()
+            except OSError:
+                pass
+
+    def _read_messages(self) -> None:
+        try:
+            while True:
+                message = read_frame(self._stdout)
+                if message is None:
+                    self._messages.put(_TransportClosed(None))
+                    return
+                self._messages.put(message)
+        except BaseException as exc:
+            self._messages.put(_TransportClosed(exc))
+
+    def _read_stderr(self) -> None:
+        try:
+            while True:
+                # 直接读 pipe fd，兼容 Popen(bufsize=0) 返回的 FileIO，也
+                # 保证短小启动诊断不必凑满 4 KiB 才进入 tail。
+                chunk = os.read(self._stderr.fileno(), 4096)
+                if not chunk:
+                    return
+                with self._stderr_lock:
+                    self._stderr_tail.extend(chunk)
+                    if len(self._stderr_tail) > _STDERR_TAIL_BYTES:
+                        del self._stderr_tail[:-_STDERR_TAIL_BYTES]
+        except OSError:
+            return
diff --git a/silitok_speed/runtimes/freetoken/worker.py b/silitok_speed/runtimes/freetoken/worker.py

index 00000000..d54a1ef0

+"""由 Speed 启动的 FreeToken Qwen3.x 单模型 worker。
+
+worker 与父进程共享同一 Python 环境和发布物，但用进程生命周期吸收
+FreeToken 每进程只能创建一次 Engine 的约束。stdout 专用于版本化协议，
+FreeToken 日志与 traceback 全部写 stderr。
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import queue
+import sys
+import threading
+import traceback
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+from .engine_bridge import (
+    _EngineReplyFailure as _EngineReplyFailure,
+    _WorkerShutdown as _WorkerShutdown,
+    _build_llm,
+    _build_streaming_llm_class as _build_streaming_llm_class,
+    _llm_kwargs as _llm_kwargs,
+    _prompt_tokens as _prompt_tokens,
+    _sampling_params as _sampling_params,
+    _startup_log,
+    _worker_options as _worker_options,
+)
+
+
+@dataclass
+class _RequestControl:
+    request_id: str
+    cancelled: threading.Event
+
+
+class _WorkerState:
+    """控制线程与唯一 CUDA runtime 线程之间的小型同步面。"""
+
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self.commands: queue.Queue[dict[str, Any] | None] = queue.Queue()
+        self.reader_error: BaseException | None = None
+        self._active: _RequestControl | None = None
+        self._queued_request_ids: set[str] = set()
+        self._pending_cancels: set[str] = set()
+        self._lock = threading.Lock()
+
+    def set_active(self, control: _RequestControl | None) -> None:
+        with self._lock:
+            self._active = control
+            if control is not None:
+                self._queued_request_ids.discard(control.request_id)
+                if control.request_id in self._pending_cancels:
+                    self._pending_cancels.discard(control.request_id)
+                    control.cancelled.set()
+
+    def enqueue(self, command: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
+        """先登记待执行请求，再交给 runtime 线程，封住早到取消竞态。"""
+
+        if command.get("type") == "generate":
+            request_id = command.get("request_id")
+            if not isinstance(request_id, str) or not request_id:
+                raise ValueError("worker request_id must be a non-empty string")
+            with self._lock:
+                self._queued_request_ids.add(request_id)
+        self.commands.put(command)
+
+    def finish(self, control: _RequestControl) -> None:
+        """结束一个请求并丢弃只属于该请求的暂存取消。"""
+
+        with self._lock:
+            if self._active is control:
+                self._active = None
+            self._queued_request_ids.discard(control.request_id)
+            self._pending_cancels.discard(control.request_id)
+
+    def cancel(self, request_id: object | None = None) -> None:
+        with self._lock:
+            active = self._active
+            if active is not None and (
+                request_id is None or request_id == active.request_id
+            ):
+                active.cancelled.set()
+            elif isinstance(request_id, str) and request_id in self._queued_request_ids:
+                self._pending_cancels.add(request_id)
+
+
+def _bootstrap_package_path() -> None:
+    """在 ``-I`` 模式下只加入承载当前 worker 文件的发布物根。"""
+
+    package_parent = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3])
+    if package_parent not in sys.path:
+        sys.path.insert(0, package_parent)
+
+
+def _redirect_stdout_to_protocol():
+    """保留私有协议句柄，将Python、原生库及后代进程的stdout统一送到stderr。"""
+
+    stdout_fd, stderr_fd = sys.stdout.fileno(), sys.stderr.fileno()
+    protocol = os.fdopen(os.dup(stdout_fd), "wb", buffering=0)
+    os.set_inheritable(protocol.fileno(), False)
+    sys.stdout.flush()
+    os.dup2(stderr_fd, stdout_fd)
+    if os.name == "nt":
+        import ctypes
+        import msvcrt
+        from ctypes import wintypes
+
+        # CRT描述符和Win32标准句柄独立；只改Python对象不能隔离JIT及子进程输出。
+        set_stdout = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True).SetStdHandle
+        set_stdout.argtypes = [wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.HANDLE]
+        set_stdout.restype = wintypes.BOOL
+        if not set_stdout(-11, msvcrt.get_osfhandle(stderr_fd)):
+            raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
+    sys.stdout = sys.stderr
+    return protocol
+
+
+def _command_reader(state: _WorkerState, stream) -> None:
+    """持续读取父进程命令；取消仅设置 Event，不接触 FreeToken/CUDA。"""
+
+    from silitok_speed.runtimes.freetoken.protocol import read_frame
+
+    try:
+        _startup_log("command reader ready")
+        while True:
+            command = read_frame(stream)
+            if command is None:
+                state.cancel()
+                state.commands.put(None)
+                return
+            command_type = command.get("type")
+            if command_type == "cancel":
+                state.cancel(command.get("request_id"))
+                continue
+            if command_type == "shutdown":
+                state.cancel()
+                state.commands.put(command)
+                return
+            state.enqueue(command)
+    except BaseException as exc:
+        state.reader_error = exc
+        state.cancel()
+        state.commands.put(None)
+
+
+def _runtime_main(
+    state: _WorkerState,
+    writer,
+    load_command: dict[str, Any],
+    command_stream,
+) -> int:
+    """在进程主线程持有 FreeToken 的完整 load/generate/shutdown 生命周期。"""
+
+    llm, SamplingParams, torch, identity = _build_llm(load_command, writer)
+    from freetoken.kvcache.cache_status import compute_cache_pools
+
+    reader = threading.Thread(
+        target=_command_reader,
+        args=(state, command_stream),
+        name="silitok-freetoken-control",
+        daemon=True,
+    )
+    reader.start()
+    ready = {
+        "type": "ready",
+        "kernel_artifacts": identity.artifact_count,
+        "vram_bytes": int(torch.cuda.memory_reserved(0)),
+        "cache_geometry": compute_cache_pools(llm.engine),
+    }
+    storage = _expert_storage(llm.engine)
+    if storage:
+        ready["expert_storage"] = storage
+    from silitok_speed.runtimes.freetoken.mtp import actual_mtp_ready
+
+    ready["mtp"] = actual_mtp_ready(llm.engine)
+    writer.send(ready)
+    try:
+        llm.serve_forever(state, SamplingParams)
+    finally:
+        llm.shutdown()
+    writer.send({"type": "stopped"})
+    return 0
+
+
+def _expert_storage(engine) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    """汇总实际 host bank 字节与锁页状态，用于全量压缩专家的验收回执。"""
+    cache = getattr(engine, "moe_offload_cache", None)
+    if cache is None:
+        return {}
+    tensors = [
+        tensor for per_layer in cache.bank_sources.values() for tensor in per_layer
+    ]
+    ownership = getattr(cache, "protected_payload", None)
+    result = {
+        "format": cache.quant_format,
+        "layers": cache.num_layers,
+        "experts_per_layer": cache.num_experts,
+        "host_bytes": sum(tensor.nbytes for tensor in tensors),
+        "all_pinned": all(tensor.is_pinned() for tensor in tensors if tensor.numel()),
+        "gpu_cache_bytes": sum(tensor.nbytes for tensor in cache.bank_caches.values()),
+    }
+    if ownership is not None:
+        result.update(
+            protected_experts=len(ownership.protected_slots),
+            dynamic_slots=ownership.dynamic,
+            protected_gpu_bytes=ownership.gpu_bank_bytes
+            // ownership.slots
+            * len(ownership.protected_slots),
+        )
+    return result
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    """建立协议通道、启动只读控制线程，并运行唯一 CUDA runtime。"""
+
+    _bootstrap_package_path()
+    if len(sys.argv) >= 2 and sys.argv[1] == "--self-check":
+        from silitok_speed.qwen36.freetoken_release import main as release_main
+
+        return release_main(["self-check", *sys.argv[2:]])
+    protocol_stream = _redirect_stdout_to_protocol()
+    from silitok_speed.runtimes.freetoken.protocol import FrameWriter, read_frame
+
+    writer = FrameWriter(protocol_stream)
+    state = _WorkerState()
+    writer.send(
+        {
+            "type": "hello",
+            "pid": os.getpid(),
+            "python_executable": str(Path(sys.executable).resolve()),
+        }
+    )
+    try:
+        _startup_log("runtime waiting for load command")
+        load_command = read_frame(sys.stdin.buffer)
+        if load_command is None:
+            raise EOFError("Speed closed the FreeToken worker command pipe")
+        if load_command.get("type") != "load":
+            raise ValueError("expected 'load' as the first worker command")
+        _startup_log("runtime accepted load command")
+        return _runtime_main(state, writer, load_command, sys.stdin.buffer)
+    except BaseException:
+        traceback.print_exc(file=sys.stderr)
+        try:
+            writer.send(
+                {
+                    "type": "fatal",
+                    "code": "freetoken_worker_failed",
+                    "message": "FreeToken worker stopped after an internal failure",
+                }
+            )
+        except BaseException:
+            pass
+        return 1
+    finally:
+        protocol_stream.close()
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())
